#CELL 14.2 - Improved experimental helpers, prompting strategies, evaluation, and artifact-saving utilities

import json
import time
from copy import deepcopy
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import pandas as pd
import hashlib

def validate_section_14_metric_name(metric_name: str) -> None:
    supported_metric_names = {"bleu_score", "chrf_score", "exact_match_accuracy"}
    if metric_name not in supported_metric_names:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unsupported Section 14 metric name: {metric_name}. "
            f"Supported metrics: {sorted(supported_metric_names)}"
        )


def section_14_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
    summary_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    return json.loads(summary_dataframe.to_json(orient="records", force_ascii=False))


def section_14_extract_usage_metadata_summary(
    response: Any,
) -> Dict[str, Optional[int]]:
    if "extract_usage_metadata_summary" in globals():
        return extract_usage_metadata_summary(response)

    usage_metadata = getattr(response, "usage_metadata", None)

    return {
        "prompt_token_count": getattr(usage_metadata, "prompt_token_count", None),
        "candidates_token_count": getattr(
            usage_metadata,
            "candidates_token_count",
            None,
        ),
        "thoughts_token_count": getattr(usage_metadata, "thoughts_token_count", None),
        "cached_content_token_count": getattr(
            usage_metadata,
            "cached_content_token_count",
            None,
        ),
        "total_token_count": getattr(usage_metadata, "total_token_count", None),
    }


def section_14_count_input_tokens_for_prompt(
    gemma_api_client: Any,
    model_id: str,
    prompt_text: str,
) -> Optional[int]:
    if "count_input_tokens_for_prompt" in globals():
        return count_input_tokens_for_prompt(
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            prompt_text=prompt_text,
        )

    if gemma_api_client is None:
        return None

    try:
        count_response = gemma_api_client.models.count_tokens(
            model=model_id,
            contents=prompt_text,
        )

        if hasattr(count_response, "total_tokens"):
            return int(count_response.total_tokens)

        if isinstance(count_response, dict) and "total_tokens" in count_response:
            return int(count_response["total_tokens"])
    except Exception:
        return None

    return None


def get_problem_record_by_name(
    selected_problem_subset: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    requested_problem_name: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    requested_problem_key = canonicalize_problem_name(requested_problem_name)

    for problem_record in selected_problem_subset:
        if problem_record["name_key"] == requested_problem_key:
            return deepcopy(problem_record)

    raise ValueError(
        f"Problem '{requested_problem_name}' was not found in the selected subset."
    )


def get_zero_shot_problem_result_by_name(
    zero_shot_problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    problem_name: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    normalized_problem_name = canonicalize_problem_name(problem_name)

    for problem_result in zero_shot_problem_results:
        if canonicalize_problem_name(problem_result["problem_name"]) == normalized_problem_name:
            return deepcopy(problem_result)

    raise ValueError(
        f"Baseline result for problem '{problem_name}' was not found."
    )


def build_support_examples_block(problem_record: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        f"{example_index}. {example_text}"
        for example_index, example_text in enumerate(problem_record["data"], start=1)
    )


def build_questions_block(problem_record: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        f"{question_index}. {question_text}"
        for question_index, question_text in enumerate(
            problem_record["questions"],
            start=1,
        )
    )


def build_linguistic_toolbox_block() -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        f"- {guideline}"
        for guideline in IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_LINGUISTIC_TOOLBOX_GUIDELINES
    )


def build_final_json_contract_block(problem_record: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return (
        f"The JSON object must have exactly this schema:\n"
        f"{json.dumps(ZERO_SHOT_BASELINE_JSON_RESPONSE_EXAMPLE, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)}\n\n"
        f"Important rules:\n"
        f"- Return exactly {problem_record['num_questions']} answers.\n"
        f"- Keep the answers in the same order as the questions.\n"
        f'- Preserve the expected label style used by the dataset, such as "English:" or the target-language label.\n'
        f"- Do not add explanations.\n"
        f"- Do not add comments.\n"
        f"- Return only a valid JSON object."
    )


def build_explicit_rule_extraction_analysis_prompt(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
    support_examples_block = build_support_examples_block(problem_record)
    linguistic_toolbox_block = build_linguistic_toolbox_block()

    prompt_text = f"""
You are solving a linguistic olympiad problem.

Problem name: {problem_record["name"]}
Task family: {", ".join(problem_record["type"])}
Number of support examples: {problem_record["num_support_examples"]}
Number of questions to solve later: {problem_record["num_questions"]}

This is an analysis stage only.
Do not answer the questions yet.

Use the following linguistic checklist whenever it is supported by the data:
{linguistic_toolbox_block}

Produce a compact analysis memo with exactly these sections:
1. Candidate lexicon and stable phrase correspondences
2. Word order and constituency patterns
3. Morphology or recurrent markers
4. Translation procedure for unseen questions
5. Remaining ambiguities or risk points

Support examples:
{support_examples_block}
""".strip()

    return prompt_text


def build_explicit_rule_extraction_final_prompt(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    analysis_memo_text: str,
) -> str:
    support_examples_block = build_support_examples_block(problem_record)
    questions_block = build_questions_block(problem_record)
    final_json_contract_block = build_final_json_contract_block(problem_record)

    prompt_text = f"""
You are solving a linguistic olympiad problem.

Problem name: {problem_record["name"]}
Task family: {", ".join(problem_record["type"])}

Use the support examples and the analysis memo below to answer the questions.

{final_json_contract_block}

Support examples:
{support_examples_block}

Analysis memo:
{analysis_memo_text.strip()}

Questions to solve:
{questions_block}
""".strip()

    return prompt_text


def build_recursive_bootstrapping_analysis_prompt(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
    support_examples_block = build_support_examples_block(problem_record)
    linguistic_toolbox_block = build_linguistic_toolbox_block()

    prompt_text = f"""
You are solving a linguistic olympiad problem.

Problem name: {problem_record["name"]}
Task family: {", ".join(problem_record["type"])}
Number of support examples: {problem_record["num_support_examples"]}
Number of questions to solve later: {problem_record["num_questions"]}

This is a recursive bootstrapping stage.
Do not answer the questions yet.

Reason from simpler correspondences to harder ones.
Only promote a rule when it is supported by the examples.

Use the following checklist whenever relevant:
{linguistic_toolbox_block}

Produce a compact working memo with exactly these sections:
1. Simplest support examples and why they are simplest
2. Mini-lexicon built from those simple examples
3. Compositional rules for larger phrases
4. Most reliable sentence template(s)
5. Minimal translation algorithm for the final questions

Support examples:
{support_examples_block}
""".strip()

    return prompt_text


def build_recursive_bootstrapping_final_prompt(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    bootstrapping_memo_text: str,
) -> str:
    support_examples_block = build_support_examples_block(problem_record)
    questions_block = build_questions_block(problem_record)
    final_json_contract_block = build_final_json_contract_block(problem_record)

    prompt_text = f"""
You are solving a linguistic olympiad problem.

Problem name: {problem_record["name"]}
Task family: {", ".join(problem_record["type"])}

Use the support examples and the recursive bootstrapping memo below to answer the questions.

{final_json_contract_block}

Support examples:
{support_examples_block}

Recursive bootstrapping memo:
{bootstrapping_memo_text.strip()}

Questions to solve:
{questions_block}
""".strip()

    return prompt_text


def build_generator_candidate_prompt(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
    support_examples_block = build_support_examples_block(problem_record)
    questions_block = build_questions_block(problem_record)
    linguistic_toolbox_block = build_linguistic_toolbox_block()

    prompt_text = f"""
You are solving a linguistic olympiad problem.

Problem name: {problem_record["name"]}
Task family: {", ".join(problem_record["type"])}

This is the generator stage of a generator-critic workflow.
Produce a preliminary solution draft, not the final JSON answer yet.

Use the following checklist whenever relevant:
{linguistic_toolbox_block}

For each question, provide:
- a tentative answer,
- the support examples or correspondences that justify it,
- the main uncertainty or possible error risk.

Also end with:
- global rule hypotheses,
- likely failure points.

Support examples:
{support_examples_block}

Questions to solve:
{questions_block}
""".strip()

    return prompt_text


def build_generator_critic_prompt(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    generator_draft_text: str,
) -> str:
    support_examples_block = build_support_examples_block(problem_record)
    questions_block = build_questions_block(problem_record)

    prompt_text = f"""
You are the critic of a preliminary linguistic olympiad solution.

Problem name: {problem_record["name"]}
Task family: {", ".join(problem_record["type"])}

Inspect the support examples, the questions, and the candidate draft below.
Your job is to detect inconsistencies, unsupported mappings, word-order mistakes,
morphology mistakes, and label-style mismatches.

Produce a concise correction memo with exactly these sections:
1. Confirmed rules
2. Detected mistakes or unsupported assumptions
3. Concrete answer-level corrections
4. Final recommendations for revision

Support examples:
{support_examples_block}

Questions to solve:
{questions_block}

Candidate draft from the generator:
{generator_draft_text.strip()}
""".strip()

    return prompt_text


def build_generator_critic_final_prompt(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    generator_draft_text: str,
    critic_memo_text: str,
) -> str:
    support_examples_block = build_support_examples_block(problem_record)
    questions_block = build_questions_block(problem_record)
    final_json_contract_block = build_final_json_contract_block(problem_record)

    prompt_text = f"""
You are revising a linguistic olympiad solution after critique.

Problem name: {problem_record["name"]}
Task family: {", ".join(problem_record["type"])}

Use the support examples, the generator draft, and the critic memo to produce the final answers.

{final_json_contract_block}

Support examples:
{support_examples_block}

Generator draft:
{generator_draft_text.strip()}

Critic memo:
{critic_memo_text.strip()}

Questions to solve:
{questions_block}
""".strip()

    return prompt_text


def normalize_text_generation_backend_output(
    backend_output: Any,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(backend_output, str):
        return {
            "raw_model_response": backend_output,
            "request_attempts": 1,
            "request_elapsed_seconds": 0.0,
            "counted_input_tokens": None,
            "prompt_token_count": None,
            "candidates_token_count": None,
            "thoughts_token_count": None,
            "cached_content_token_count": None,
            "total_token_count": None,
        }

    if not isinstance(backend_output, dict):
        raise ValueError(
            "The custom text_generation_backend must return either a string "
            "or a dictionary."
        )

    normalized_output = {
        "raw_model_response": backend_output.get(
            "raw_model_response",
            backend_output.get("text", ""),
        ),
        "request_attempts": backend_output.get("request_attempts", 1),
        "request_elapsed_seconds": backend_output.get("request_elapsed_seconds", 0.0),
        "counted_input_tokens": backend_output.get("counted_input_tokens"),
        "prompt_token_count": backend_output.get("prompt_token_count"),
        "candidates_token_count": backend_output.get("candidates_token_count"),
        "thoughts_token_count": backend_output.get("thoughts_token_count"),
        "cached_content_token_count": backend_output.get("cached_content_token_count"),
        "total_token_count": backend_output.get("total_token_count"),
    }

    return normalized_output


def request_raw_text_from_gemma(
    prompt_text: str,
    gemma_api_client: Any,
    model_id: str,
    temperature: float,
    top_p: float,
    max_api_retries: int,
    request_pause_seconds: float,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if gemma_api_client is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "Gemma client is not configured. Set GEMINI_API_KEY and rerun the global configuration cell."
        )

    counted_input_tokens = section_14_count_input_tokens_for_prompt(
        gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
        model_id=model_id,
        prompt_text=prompt_text,
    )

    last_exception: Optional[Exception] = None

    for attempt_number in range(1, max_api_retries + 1):
        request_started_at = time.perf_counter()

        try:
            response = gemma_api_client.models.generate_content(
                model=model_id,
                contents=prompt_text,
                config=genai.types.GenerateContentConfig(
                    temperature=temperature,
                    top_p=top_p,
                ),
            )

            request_elapsed_seconds = time.perf_counter() - request_started_at
            raw_model_response_text = getattr(response, "text", None) or ""

            usage_metadata_summary = section_14_extract_usage_metadata_summary(response)

            return {
                "raw_model_response": raw_model_response_text,
                "request_attempts": attempt_number,
                "request_elapsed_seconds": request_elapsed_seconds,
                "counted_input_tokens": counted_input_tokens,
                **usage_metadata_summary,
            }

        except Exception as exc:
            last_exception = exc

            if attempt_number == max_api_retries:
                break

            retry_sleep_seconds = max(
                request_pause_seconds,
                2 ** (attempt_number - 1),
            )
            print(
                f"Improved-run request failed "
                f"(attempt {attempt_number}/{max_api_retries}): {exc}"
            )
            print(f"Retrying after {retry_sleep_seconds:.1f} seconds...")
            time.sleep(retry_sleep_seconds)

    raise RuntimeError(
        "Failed to obtain a valid text response for the improved experimental run."
    ) from last_exception

def execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
    stage_name: str,
    prompt_text: str,
    strategy_specification: Dict[str, Any],
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    gemma_api_client: Optional[Any] = None,
    model_id: str = MODEL_ID,
    text_generation_backend: Optional[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], Any]] = None,
    request_pause_seconds: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_REQUEST_PAUSE_SECONDS,
    temperature: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TEMPERATURE,
    top_p: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TOP_P,
    max_api_retries: int = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_MAX_API_RETRIES,
    expects_json_answers: bool = False,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    stage_record = {
        "stage_name": stage_name,
        "strategy_name": strategy_specification["strategy_name"],
        "strategy_version": strategy_specification["strategy_version"],
        "problem_name": problem_record["name"],
        "prompt_text": prompt_text,
        "prompt_character_count": len(prompt_text),
        "expects_json_answers": expects_json_answers,
    }

    if text_generation_backend is None:
        if expects_json_answers:
            backend_output = request_final_json_predictions_from_gemma(
                problem_record=problem_record,
                prompt_text=prompt_text,
                gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
                model_id=model_id,
                temperature=temperature,
                top_p=top_p,
                max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
                request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            )
        else:
            backend_output = request_raw_text_from_gemma(
                prompt_text=prompt_text,
                gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
                model_id=model_id,
                temperature=temperature,
                top_p=top_p,
                max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
                request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            )
    else:
        backend_output = normalize_text_generation_backend_output(
            text_generation_backend(stage_record)
        )
        if expects_json_answers:
            backend_output["predicted_answers"] = parse_zero_shot_predicted_answers(
                model_response_text=backend_output["raw_model_response"],
                expected_num_answers=problem_record["num_questions"],
            )

    stage_record.update(backend_output)
    return stage_record

def request_final_json_predictions_from_gemma(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    prompt_text: str,
    gemma_api_client: Any,
    model_id: str,
    temperature: float,
    top_p: float,
    max_api_retries: int,
    request_pause_seconds: float,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if gemma_api_client is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "Gemma client is not configured. Set GEMINI_API_KEY and rerun the global configuration cell."
        )

    counted_input_tokens = section_14_count_input_tokens_for_prompt(
        gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
        model_id=model_id,
        prompt_text=prompt_text,
    )

    last_exception: Optional[Exception] = None

    for attempt_number in range(1, max_api_retries + 1):
        request_started_at = time.perf_counter()

        try:
            response = gemma_api_client.models.generate_content(
                model=model_id,
                contents=prompt_text,
                config=genai.types.GenerateContentConfig(
                    temperature=temperature,
                    top_p=top_p,
                ),
            )

            request_elapsed_seconds = time.perf_counter() - request_started_at
            raw_model_response_text = getattr(response, "text", None) or ""

            predicted_answers = parse_zero_shot_predicted_answers(
                model_response_text=raw_model_response_text,
                expected_num_answers=problem_record["num_questions"],
            )

            usage_metadata_summary = section_14_extract_usage_metadata_summary(response)

            return {
                "raw_model_response": raw_model_response_text,
                "predicted_answers": predicted_answers,
                "request_attempts": attempt_number,
                "request_elapsed_seconds": request_elapsed_seconds,
                "counted_input_tokens": counted_input_tokens,
                **usage_metadata_summary,
            }

        except Exception as exc:
            last_exception = exc

            if attempt_number == max_api_retries:
                break

            retry_sleep_seconds = max(
                request_pause_seconds,
                2 ** (attempt_number - 1),
            )
            print(
                f"Improved-run final JSON request failed for problem "
                f"'{problem_record['name']}' "
                f"(attempt {attempt_number}/{max_api_retries}): {exc}"
            )
            print(f"Retrying after {retry_sleep_seconds:.1f} seconds...")
            time.sleep(retry_sleep_seconds)

    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Failed to obtain a valid final JSON response for problem "
        f"'{problem_record['name']}' after {max_api_retries} attempts."
    ) from last_exception


def sum_stage_numeric_field(
    stage_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    field_name: str,
) -> Optional[float]:
    numeric_values = [
        stage_record[field_name]
        for stage_record in stage_records
        if stage_record.get(field_name) is not None
    ]

    if not numeric_values:
        return None

    return float(sum(numeric_values))

def run_improved_prompting_strategy_for_problem(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    strategy_specification: Dict[str, Any],
    baseline_problem_result: Dict[str, Any],
    gemma_api_client: Optional[Any] = None,
    model_id: str = MODEL_ID,
    text_generation_backend: Optional[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], Any]] = None,
    request_pause_seconds: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_REQUEST_PAUSE_SECONDS,
    temperature: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TEMPERATURE,
    top_p: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TOP_P,
    max_api_retries: int = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_MAX_API_RETRIES,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    strategy_name = strategy_specification["strategy_name"]
    using_default_gemma_backend = text_generation_backend is None

    stage_records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    if strategy_name == "explicit_rule_extraction_two_pass":
        analysis_prompt_text = build_explicit_rule_extraction_analysis_prompt(
            problem_record
        )
        analysis_stage_record = execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
            stage_name="rule_extraction_analysis",
            prompt_text=analysis_prompt_text,
            strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            problem_record=problem_record,
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
            request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
            max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
        )
        stage_records.append(analysis_stage_record)

        if using_default_gemma_backend:
            time.sleep(request_pause_seconds)

        final_prompt_text = build_explicit_rule_extraction_final_prompt(
            problem_record=problem_record,
            analysis_memo_text=analysis_stage_record["raw_model_response"],
        )
        final_stage_record = execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
            stage_name="final_json_answers",
            prompt_text=final_prompt_text,
            strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            problem_record=problem_record,
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
            request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
            max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
            expects_json_answers=True,
        )
        stage_records.append(final_stage_record)

    elif strategy_name == "recursive_bootstrapping_two_pass":
        bootstrapping_prompt_text = build_recursive_bootstrapping_analysis_prompt(
            problem_record
        )
        bootstrapping_stage_record = execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
            stage_name="recursive_bootstrapping_analysis",
            prompt_text=bootstrapping_prompt_text,
            strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            problem_record=problem_record,
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
            request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
            max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
        )
        stage_records.append(bootstrapping_stage_record)

        if using_default_gemma_backend:
            time.sleep(request_pause_seconds)

        final_prompt_text = build_recursive_bootstrapping_final_prompt(
            problem_record=problem_record,
            bootstrapping_memo_text=bootstrapping_stage_record["raw_model_response"],
        )
        final_stage_record = execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
            stage_name="final_json_answers",
            prompt_text=final_prompt_text,
            strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            problem_record=problem_record,
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
            request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
            max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
            expects_json_answers=True,
        )
        stage_records.append(final_stage_record)

    elif strategy_name == "generator_critic_three_pass":
        generator_prompt_text = build_generator_candidate_prompt(problem_record)
        generator_stage_record = execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
            stage_name="generator_draft",
            prompt_text=generator_prompt_text,
            strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            problem_record=problem_record,
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
            request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
            max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
        )
        stage_records.append(generator_stage_record)

        if using_default_gemma_backend:
            time.sleep(request_pause_seconds)

        critic_prompt_text = build_generator_critic_prompt(
            problem_record=problem_record,
            generator_draft_text=generator_stage_record["raw_model_response"],
        )
        critic_stage_record = execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
            stage_name="critic_memo",
            prompt_text=critic_prompt_text,
            strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            problem_record=problem_record,
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
            request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
            max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
        )
        stage_records.append(critic_stage_record)

        if using_default_gemma_backend:
            time.sleep(request_pause_seconds)

        final_prompt_text = build_generator_critic_final_prompt(
            problem_record=problem_record,
            generator_draft_text=generator_stage_record["raw_model_response"],
            critic_memo_text=critic_stage_record["raw_model_response"],
        )
        final_stage_record = execute_improved_reasoning_stage(
            stage_name="final_json_answers",
            prompt_text=final_prompt_text,
            strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            problem_record=problem_record,
            gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
            model_id=model_id,
            text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
            request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
            max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
            expects_json_answers=True,
        )
        stage_records.append(final_stage_record)

    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported improved strategy: {strategy_name}")

    predicted_answers = final_stage_record["predicted_answers"]

    reference_answers = [
        normalize_linguistic_answer_text(answer_text)
        for answer_text in problem_record["answers"]
    ]

    metric_results = compute_problem_level_bleu_and_chrf(
        reference_answers=reference_answers,
        predicted_answers=predicted_answers,
    )

    total_request_attempts = int(
        sum(stage_record["request_attempts"] for stage_record in stage_records)
    )

    total_counted_input_tokens = sum_stage_numeric_field(
        stage_records=stage_records,
        field_name="counted_input_tokens",
    )
    total_prompt_token_count = sum_stage_numeric_field(
        stage_records=stage_records,
        field_name="prompt_token_count",
    )
    total_candidates_token_count = sum_stage_numeric_field(
        stage_records=stage_records,
        field_name="candidates_token_count",
    )
    total_thoughts_token_count = sum_stage_numeric_field(
        stage_records=stage_records,
        field_name="thoughts_token_count",
    )
    total_cached_content_token_count = sum_stage_numeric_field(
        stage_records=stage_records,
        field_name="cached_content_token_count",
    )
    total_token_count = sum_stage_numeric_field(
        stage_records=stage_records,
        field_name="total_token_count",
    )
    total_request_elapsed_seconds = sum_stage_numeric_field(
        stage_records=stage_records,
        field_name="request_elapsed_seconds",
    )

    baseline_bleu_score = float(baseline_problem_result["bleu_score"])
    baseline_chrf_score = float(baseline_problem_result["chrf_score"])
    baseline_exact_match_accuracy = float(
        baseline_problem_result["exact_match_accuracy"]
    )

    return {
        "evaluation_mode": "improved_experimental_run",
        "problem_name": problem_record["name"],
        "subset_order": problem_record["subset_order"],
        "task_family": list(problem_record["type"]),
        "difficulty": problem_record["difficulty"],
        "subset_group": problem_record["subset_group"],
        "selection_role": problem_record["selection_role"],
        "reasoning_focus": problem_record["reasoning_focus"],
        "num_support_examples": problem_record["num_support_examples"],
        "num_questions": problem_record["num_questions"],
        "strategy_name": strategy_specification["strategy_name"],
        "strategy_version": strategy_specification["strategy_version"],
        "procedure_type": strategy_specification["procedure_type"],
        "num_passes": int(strategy_specification["num_passes"]),
        "analytical_role": strategy_specification["analytical_role"],
        "questions": list(problem_record["questions"]),
        "reference_answers": reference_answers,
        "predicted_answers": predicted_answers,
        "stage_records": stage_records,
        "final_raw_model_response": final_stage_record["raw_model_response"],
        "total_request_attempts": total_request_attempts,
        "total_counted_input_tokens": total_counted_input_tokens,
        "total_prompt_token_count": total_prompt_token_count,
        "total_candidates_token_count": total_candidates_token_count,
        "total_thoughts_token_count": total_thoughts_token_count,
        "total_cached_content_token_count": total_cached_content_token_count,
        "total_token_count": total_token_count,
        "total_request_elapsed_seconds": total_request_elapsed_seconds,
        "baseline_bleu_score": baseline_bleu_score,
        "baseline_chrf_score": baseline_chrf_score,
        "baseline_exact_match_accuracy": baseline_exact_match_accuracy,
        "bleu_score": float(metric_results["bleu_score"]),
        "chrf_score": float(metric_results["chrf_score"]),
        "exact_match_accuracy": float(metric_results["exact_match_accuracy"]),
        "bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline": float(
            metric_results["bleu_score"] - baseline_bleu_score
        ),
        "chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline": float(
            metric_results["chrf_score"] - baseline_chrf_score
        ),
        "exact_match_accuracy_delta_vs_baseline": float(
            metric_results["exact_match_accuracy"] - baseline_exact_match_accuracy
        ),
    }


def run_improved_experimental_suite(
    target_problem_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    strategy_specifications: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    zero_shot_problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    gemma_api_client: Optional[Any] = None,
    model_id: str = MODEL_ID,
    text_generation_backend: Optional[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], Any]] = None,
    request_pause_seconds: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_REQUEST_PAUSE_SECONDS,
    temperature: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TEMPERATURE,
    top_p: float = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TOP_P,
    max_api_retries: int = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_MAX_API_RETRIES,
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not target_problem_records:
        raise ValueError("target_problem_records cannot be empty.")

    enabled_strategy_specifications = [
        strategy_specification
        for strategy_specification in strategy_specifications
        if strategy_specification.get("is_enabled", True)
    ]

    if not enabled_strategy_specifications:
        raise ValueError("No improved prompting strategies are enabled.")

    baseline_result_lookup = {
        problem_result["problem_name"]: problem_result
        for problem_result in zero_shot_problem_results
    }

    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    total_runs = len(target_problem_records) * len(enabled_strategy_specifications)
    current_run_index = 0

    for target_problem_record in target_problem_records:
        baseline_problem_result = baseline_result_lookup.get(target_problem_record["name"])
        if baseline_problem_result is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"No baseline result found for target problem '{target_problem_record['name']}'."
            )

        for strategy_specification in enabled_strategy_specifications:
            current_run_index += 1

            improved_run_result = run_improved_prompting_strategy_for_problem(
                problem_record=target_problem_record,
                strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
                baseline_problem_result=baseline_problem_result,
                gemma_api_client=gemma_api_client,
                model_id=model_id,
                text_generation_backend=text_generation_backend,
                request_pause_seconds=request_pause_seconds,
                temperature=temperature,
                top_p=top_p,
                max_api_retries=max_api_retries,
            )
            improved_experimental_results.append(improved_run_result)

            print(
                f"[{current_run_index}/{total_runs}] "
                f"{target_problem_record['name']} | "
                f"{strategy_specification['strategy_name']} -> "
                f"BLEU: {improved_run_result['bleu_score']:.2f} "
                f"(Δ {improved_run_result['bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline']:+.2f}) | "
                f"chrF: {improved_run_result['chrf_score']:.2f} "
                f"(Δ {improved_run_result['chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline']:+.2f}) | "
                f"Exact match: {improved_run_result['exact_match_accuracy']:.2f}"
            )

            if text_generation_backend is None and current_run_index < total_runs:
                time.sleep(request_pause_seconds)

    return improved_experimental_results


def classify_improvement_strength(
    chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline: float,
) -> str:
    if chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline >= 10.0:
        return "clear"
    if chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline >= 3.0:
        return "modest"
    if chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline > -3.0:
        return "negligible"
    return "negative"


def build_improved_experimental_summary_dataframe(
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if not improved_experimental_results:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build the improved experimental summary dataframe from an empty result list."
        )

    summary_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for improved_run_result in improved_experimental_results:
        summary_rows.append(
            {
                "problem_name": improved_run_result["problem_name"],
                "subset_order": improved_run_result["subset_order"],
                "strategy_name": improved_run_result["strategy_name"],
                "strategy_version": improved_run_result["strategy_version"],
                "procedure_type": improved_run_result["procedure_type"],
                "num_passes": improved_run_result["num_passes"],
                "analytical_role": improved_run_result["analytical_role"],
                "baseline_bleu_score": improved_run_result["baseline_bleu_score"],
                "baseline_chrf_score": improved_run_result["baseline_chrf_score"],
                "baseline_exact_match_accuracy": improved_run_result[
                    "baseline_exact_match_accuracy"
                ],
                "bleu_score": improved_run_result["bleu_score"],
                "chrf_score": improved_run_result["chrf_score"],
                "exact_match_accuracy": improved_run_result["exact_match_accuracy"],
                "bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline": improved_run_result[
                    "bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline"
                ],
                "chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline": improved_run_result[
                    "chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline"
                ],
                "exact_match_accuracy_delta_vs_baseline": improved_run_result[
                    "exact_match_accuracy_delta_vs_baseline"
                ],
                "improvement_strength": classify_improvement_strength(
                    improved_run_result["chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline"]
                ),
                "total_request_attempts": improved_run_result["total_request_attempts"],
                "total_token_count": improved_run_result["total_token_count"],
                "total_request_elapsed_seconds": improved_run_result[
                    "total_request_elapsed_seconds"
                ],
            }
        )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(summary_rows)
        .sort_values(by=["subset_order", "chrf_score", "bleu_score"], ascending=[True, False, False])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


def build_improved_vs_baseline_comparison_dataframe(
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if not improved_experimental_results:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build the improved-vs-baseline comparison dataframe from an empty result list."
        )

    comparison_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for improved_run_result in improved_experimental_results:
        comparison_rows.append(
            {
                "problem_name": improved_run_result["problem_name"],
                "strategy_name": improved_run_result["strategy_name"],
                "procedure_type": improved_run_result["procedure_type"],
                "num_passes": improved_run_result["num_passes"],
                "baseline_bleu_score": improved_run_result["baseline_bleu_score"],
                "improved_bleu_score": improved_run_result["bleu_score"],
                "bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline": improved_run_result[
                    "bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline"
                ],
                "baseline_chrf_score": improved_run_result["baseline_chrf_score"],
                "improved_chrf_score": improved_run_result["chrf_score"],
                "chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline": improved_run_result[
                    "chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline"
                ],
                "baseline_exact_match_accuracy": improved_run_result[
                    "baseline_exact_match_accuracy"
                ],
                "improved_exact_match_accuracy": improved_run_result[
                    "exact_match_accuracy"
                ],
                "exact_match_accuracy_delta_vs_baseline": improved_run_result[
                    "exact_match_accuracy_delta_vs_baseline"
                ],
                "improvement_strength": classify_improvement_strength(
                    improved_run_result["chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline"]
                ),
            }
        )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)
        .sort_values(
            by=["problem_name", "improved_chrf_score", "improved_bleu_score"],
            ascending=[True, False, False],
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


def identify_best_improved_result(
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    ranking_metric: str = "chrf_score",
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    validate_section_14_metric_name(ranking_metric)

    if not improved_experimental_results:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot identify the best improved result from an empty result list."
        )

    return max(
        improved_experimental_results,
        key=lambda result: (
            float(result[ranking_metric]),
            float(result["bleu_score"]),
            float(result["exact_match_accuracy"]),
            -int(result["num_passes"]),
        ),
    )

def build_problem_specific_strategy_interpretation_text(
    representative_result: Dict[str, Any],
    best_strategy_name: str,
) -> str:
    problem_name = representative_result["problem_name"]
    task_family_text = ", ".join(representative_result["task_family"])
    difficulty_value = int(representative_result["difficulty"])
    num_support_examples = int(representative_result["num_support_examples"])
    num_questions = int(representative_result["num_questions"])

    if "ORDER" in representative_result["task_family"] and difficulty_value >= 5:
        return (
            f"For {problem_name}, this ranking is linguistically plausible. "
            f"It is a hard {task_family_text} problem with {num_support_examples} support "
            f"examples and {num_questions} questions, so the model must stabilize local "
            f"correspondences, argument structure, and sentence-order regularities across "
            f"many items before producing full translations. This helps explain why "
            f"{best_strategy_name} is the strongest configuration, even if only marginally: "
            f"it encourages the model to move from simpler alignments toward larger "
            f"compositions instead of forcing an early global compression of the grammar. "
            f"By contrast, the explicit rule-extraction setting is more brittle on a "
            f"structurally rich problem because an overcompressed memo can lock in wrong "
            f"hypotheses about ordering or morphology, and the generator-critic workflow "
            f"adds extra cross-pass opportunities for drift when the initial draft is "
            f"already uncertain."
        )

    return (
        f"For {problem_name}, the ranking is most plausibly explained by the fact that the "
        f"problem still requires careful control of intermediate hypotheses. In that setting, "
        f"{best_strategy_name} appears to preserve the most useful task information while "
        f"avoiding the larger error propagation seen in the weaker multi-pass variants."
    )
def build_improved_experimental_runs_report_text(
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> str:
    if not improved_experimental_results:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build the improved experimental runs report from an empty result list."
        )

    summary_dataframe = build_improved_experimental_summary_dataframe(
        improved_experimental_results
    )

    report_lines = [
        "Improved experimental runs report",
        "",
    ]

    for problem_name, problem_group_dataframe in summary_dataframe.groupby(
        "problem_name",
        sort=False,
    ):
        problem_group_dataframe = problem_group_dataframe.sort_values(
            by=["chrf_score", "bleu_score"],
            ascending=[False, False],
        ).reset_index(drop=True)

        problem_result_group = [
            result
            for result in improved_experimental_results
            if result["problem_name"] == problem_name
        ]
        representative_result = problem_result_group[0]

        baseline_bleu_score = float(problem_group_dataframe.iloc[0]["baseline_bleu_score"])
        baseline_chrf_score = float(problem_group_dataframe.iloc[0]["baseline_chrf_score"])
        baseline_exact_match_accuracy = float(
            problem_group_dataframe.iloc[0]["baseline_exact_match_accuracy"]
        )

        best_strategy_row = problem_group_dataframe.iloc[0]

        report_lines.append(f"Target problem: {problem_name}")
        report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append(
            (
                f"The improved experimental phase keeps all evaluation conditions fixed "
                f"except the prompt design. For {problem_name}, the direct zero-shot baseline "
                f"was BLEU {baseline_bleu_score:.2f}, chrF {baseline_chrf_score:.2f}, and "
                f"Exact Match {baseline_exact_match_accuracy:.2f}. The runs below therefore "
                f"constitute a controlled re-evaluation of the same task, same support examples, "
                f"same questions, same references, same model, and same generation settings."
            )
        )
        report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append("Improved strategy inventory:")

        for _, strategy_row in problem_group_dataframe.iterrows():
            report_lines.append(
                f"- {strategy_row['strategy_name']} | "
                f"procedure={strategy_row['procedure_type']} | "
                f"passes={int(strategy_row['num_passes'])} | "
                f"role={strategy_row['analytical_role']} | "
                f"BLEU={strategy_row['bleu_score']:.2f} "
                f"(Δ {strategy_row['bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline']:+.2f}) | "
                f"chrF={strategy_row['chrf_score']:.2f} "
                f"(Δ {strategy_row['chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline']:+.2f}) | "
                f"ExactMatch={strategy_row['exact_match_accuracy']:.2f}"
            )

        report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append(
            (
                f"The best improved configuration for {problem_name} is "
                f"{best_strategy_row['strategy_name']}, which changes the baseline from "
                f"BLEU {baseline_bleu_score:.2f} / chrF {baseline_chrf_score:.2f} to "
                f"BLEU {best_strategy_row['bleu_score']:.2f} / chrF {best_strategy_row['chrf_score']:.2f}. "
                f"This corresponds to a "
                f"{best_strategy_row['improvement_strength']} improvement under a strictly "
                f"comparable evaluation setup."
            )
        )
        report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append(
            build_problem_specific_strategy_interpretation_text(
                representative_result=representative_result,
                best_strategy_name=best_strategy_row["strategy_name"],
            )
        )
        report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append(
            (
                "This section is valuable precisely because it tests whether better "
                "inference-time reasoning guidance, rather than retraining or dataset "
                "modification, is sufficient to improve Gemma’s performance on a difficult "
                "linguistic-olympiad problem."
            )
        )
        report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append("-" * 80)
        report_lines.append("")

    return "\n".join(report_lines).strip()

def build_enabled_strategy_records(
    strategy_specifications: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    enabled_strategy_specifications = [
        strategy_specification
        for strategy_specification in strategy_specifications
        if strategy_specification.get("is_enabled", True)
    ]

    enabled_strategy_records = [
        {
            "strategy_name": strategy_specification["strategy_name"],
            "strategy_version": strategy_specification["strategy_version"],
            "procedure_type": strategy_specification["procedure_type"],
            "num_passes": int(strategy_specification["num_passes"]),
            "analytical_role": strategy_specification["analytical_role"],
        }
        for strategy_specification in enabled_strategy_specifications
    ]

    return sorted(
        enabled_strategy_records,
        key=lambda record: (record["strategy_name"], record["strategy_version"]),
    )

def build_expected_stage_prompt_texts_for_fingerprinting(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    strategy_specification: Dict[str, Any],
) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    strategy_name = strategy_specification["strategy_name"]

    if strategy_name == "explicit_rule_extraction_two_pass":
        return [
            (
                "rule_extraction_analysis",
                build_explicit_rule_extraction_analysis_prompt(problem_record),
            ),
            (
                "final_json_answers",
                build_explicit_rule_extraction_final_prompt(
                    problem_record=problem_record,
                    analysis_memo_text="[[ANALYSIS_MEMO_PLACEHOLDER]]",
                ),
            ),
        ]

    if strategy_name == "recursive_bootstrapping_two_pass":
        return [
            (
                "recursive_bootstrapping_analysis",
                build_recursive_bootstrapping_analysis_prompt(problem_record),
            ),
            (
                "final_json_answers",
                build_recursive_bootstrapping_final_prompt(
                    problem_record=problem_record,
                    bootstrapping_memo_text="[[BOOTSTRAPPING_MEMO_PLACEHOLDER]]",
                ),
            ),
        ]

    if strategy_name == "generator_critic_three_pass":
        return [
            (
                "generator_draft",
                build_generator_candidate_prompt(problem_record),
            ),
            (
                "critic_memo",
                build_generator_critic_prompt(
                    problem_record=problem_record,
                    generator_draft_text="[[GENERATOR_DRAFT_PLACEHOLDER]]",
                ),
            ),
            (
                "final_json_answers",
                build_generator_critic_final_prompt(
                    problem_record=problem_record,
                    generator_draft_text="[[GENERATOR_DRAFT_PLACEHOLDER]]",
                    critic_memo_text="[[CRITIC_MEMO_PLACEHOLDER]]",
                ),
            ),
        ]

    raise ValueError(
        f"Unsupported improved strategy for fingerprinting: {strategy_name}"
    )

def build_improved_prompt_fingerprint_records(
    target_problem_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    strategy_specifications: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    enabled_strategy_specifications = [
        strategy_specification
        for strategy_specification in strategy_specifications
        if strategy_specification.get("is_enabled", True)
    ]

    fingerprint_records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for problem_record in sorted(
        target_problem_records,
        key=lambda record: (int(record["subset_order"]), record["name"]),
    ):
        for strategy_specification in enabled_strategy_specifications:
            stage_prompt_texts = build_expected_stage_prompt_texts_for_fingerprinting(
                problem_record=problem_record,
                strategy_specification=strategy_specification,
            )

            for stage_name, prompt_text in stage_prompt_texts:
                fingerprint_records.append(
                    {
                        "problem_name": problem_record["name"],
                        "strategy_name": strategy_specification["strategy_name"],
                        "strategy_version": strategy_specification["strategy_version"],
                        "stage_name": stage_name,
                        "prompt_character_count": len(prompt_text),
                        "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
                            prompt_text.encode("utf-8")
                        ).hexdigest(),
                    }
                )

    return fingerprint_records


def build_improved_experimental_runs_payload(
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    model_id: str,
    target_problem_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    strategy_specifications: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    summary_dataframe = build_improved_experimental_summary_dataframe(
        improved_experimental_results
    )
    comparison_dataframe = build_improved_vs_baseline_comparison_dataframe(
        improved_experimental_results
    )

    enabled_strategy_records = build_enabled_strategy_records(
        strategy_specifications
    )
    prompt_fingerprint_records = build_improved_prompt_fingerprint_records(
        target_problem_records=target_problem_records,
        strategy_specifications=strategy_specifications,
    )

    return {
        "analysis_name": "improved_experimental_runs",
        "model_id": model_id,
        "prompt_suite_version": IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_PROMPT_SUITE_VERSION,
        "evaluated_target_problem_names": sorted(
            {result["problem_name"] for result in improved_experimental_results}
        ),
        "enabled_strategy_records": enabled_strategy_records,
        "prompt_fingerprint_records": prompt_fingerprint_records,
        "improved_experimental_results": improved_experimental_results,
        "summary_records": section_14_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
            summary_dataframe
        ),
        "comparison_records": section_14_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
            comparison_dataframe
        ),
    }


def save_improved_experimental_runs_artifacts(
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    output_directory: Path,
    model_id: str,
    target_problem_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    strategy_specifications: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> Dict[str, Path]:
    if not improved_experimental_results:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot save improved experimental artifacts because the result list is empty."
        )

    output_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    summary_dataframe = build_improved_experimental_summary_dataframe(
        improved_experimental_results
    )
    comparison_dataframe = build_improved_vs_baseline_comparison_dataframe(
        improved_experimental_results
    )
    report_text = build_improved_experimental_runs_report_text(
        improved_experimental_results
    )
    payload = build_improved_experimental_runs_payload(
        improved_experimental_results=improved_experimental_results,
        model_id=model_id,
        target_problem_records=target_problem_records,
        strategy_specifications=strategy_specifications,
    )

    improved_results_json_output_path = (
        output_directory / "improved_experimental_runs_results.json"
    )
    improved_summary_csv_output_path = (
        output_directory / "improved_experimental_runs_summary.csv"
    )
    improved_comparison_csv_output_path = (
        output_directory / "improved_experimental_runs_comparison_with_baseline.csv"
    )
    improved_report_output_path = (
        output_directory / "improved_experimental_runs_report.txt"
    )

    save_json_file(payload, improved_results_json_output_path)

    summary_dataframe.to_csv(
        improved_summary_csv_output_path,
        index=False,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    comparison_dataframe.to_csv(
        improved_comparison_csv_output_path,
        index=False,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    with open(improved_report_output_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as report_file:
        report_file.write(report_text)

    return {
        "improved_results_json_output_path": improved_results_json_output_path,
        "improved_summary_csv_output_path": improved_summary_csv_output_path,
        "improved_comparison_csv_output_path": improved_comparison_csv_output_path,
        "improved_report_output_path": improved_report_output_path,
    }

def validate_saved_improved_experimental_payload(
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    expected_model_id: str,
    expected_target_problem_names: List[str],
    expected_strategy_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    expected_prompt_fingerprint_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    expected_prompt_suite_version: str,
) -> None:
    if payload.get("analysis_name") != "improved_experimental_runs":
        raise ValueError(
            "The saved artifact does not correspond to Section 14 improved experimental runs."
        )

    if payload.get("model_id") != expected_model_id:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Saved improved-run model_id={payload.get('model_id')} does not match "
            f"the current model_id={expected_model_id}."
        )

    if payload.get("prompt_suite_version") != expected_prompt_suite_version:
        raise ValueError(
            "The saved improved-run artifact was generated with a different "
            "Section 14 prompt-suite version."
        )

    saved_target_problem_names = payload.get("evaluated_target_problem_names", [])
    if sorted(saved_target_problem_names) != sorted(expected_target_problem_names):
        raise ValueError(
            "The saved improved-run artifact does not match the currently requested target problems.\n"
            f"Saved:   {saved_target_problem_names}\n"
            f"Current: {expected_target_problem_names}"
        )

    saved_strategy_records = payload.get("enabled_strategy_records", [])
    if saved_strategy_records != expected_strategy_records:
        raise ValueError(
            "The saved improved-run artifact was generated with a different "
            "enabled strategy configuration."
        )

    saved_prompt_fingerprint_records = payload.get("prompt_fingerprint_records", [])
    if saved_prompt_fingerprint_records != expected_prompt_fingerprint_records:
        raise ValueError(
            "The saved improved-run artifact was generated with different "
            "Section 14 prompt texts or prompt-building logic."
        )

    if not payload.get("improved_experimental_results"):
        raise ValueError("The saved improved-run artifact contains no result records.")
    
def load_improved_experimental_results_from_saved_artifact(
    results_json_path: Path,
    expected_model_id: str,
    expected_target_problem_names: List[str],
    expected_strategy_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    expected_prompt_fingerprint_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    expected_prompt_suite_version: str,
) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not results_json_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Saved improved experimental snapshot not found: {results_json_path}"
        )

    payload = read_json_file(results_json_path)

    validate_saved_improved_experimental_payload(
        payload=payload,
        expected_model_id=expected_model_id,
        expected_target_problem_names=expected_target_problem_names,
        expected_strategy_records=expected_strategy_records,
        expected_prompt_fingerprint_records=expected_prompt_fingerprint_records,
        expected_prompt_suite_version=expected_prompt_suite_version,
    )

    return payload["improved_experimental_results"], payload



#CELL 14.3 - Run improved experimental runs and save the corresponding artifacts

improved_experimental_target_problem_records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
improved_experimental_summary_dataframe = pd.DataFrame()
improved_experimental_comparison_dataframe = pd.DataFrame()
improved_experimental_saved_paths: Dict[str, Path] = {}
improved_experimental_saved_payload: Dict[str, Any] = {}

if not zero_shot_baseline_results:
    print(
        "Zero-shot baseline results are not available yet.\n"
        "Run CELL 8.4 and CELL 8.5 first,\n"
        "then rerun the Section 11 target-selection cell,\n"
        "and finally rerun this Section 14 execution cell."
    )
else:
    target_problem_names_to_evaluate = [IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_PRIMARY_TARGET_NAME]

    if (
        IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_EVALUATE_BACKUP_TARGET
        and IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_SECONDARY_BACKUP_NAME
        and IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_SECONDARY_BACKUP_NAME not in target_problem_names_to_evaluate
    ):
        target_problem_names_to_evaluate.append(
            IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_SECONDARY_BACKUP_NAME
        )

    improved_experimental_target_problem_records = [
        get_problem_record_by_name(
            selected_problem_subset=selected_modeling_problem_subset,
            requested_problem_name=target_problem_name,
        )
        for target_problem_name in target_problem_names_to_evaluate
    ]

    expected_improved_strategy_records = build_enabled_strategy_records(
        IMPROVED_PROMPTING_STRATEGY_SPECIFICATIONS
    )

    expected_improved_prompt_fingerprint_records = (
        build_improved_prompt_fingerprint_records(
            target_problem_records=improved_experimental_target_problem_records,
            strategy_specifications=IMPROVED_PROMPTING_STRATEGY_SPECIFICATIONS,
        )
    )

    saved_snapshot_available = IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_RESULTS_JSON_PATH.exists()
    should_run_fresh_experiments = True

    if (
        IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_REUSE_SAVED_SNAPSHOT
        and saved_snapshot_available
        and not IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_FORCE_RERUN
    ):
        try:
            (
                improved_experimental_results,
                improved_experimental_saved_payload,
            ) = load_improved_experimental_results_from_saved_artifact(
                results_json_path=IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_RESULTS_JSON_PATH,
                expected_model_id=MODEL_ID,
                expected_target_problem_names=target_problem_names_to_evaluate,
                expected_strategy_records=expected_improved_strategy_records,
                expected_prompt_fingerprint_records=expected_improved_prompt_fingerprint_records,
                expected_prompt_suite_version=IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_PROMPT_SUITE_VERSION,
            )

            should_run_fresh_experiments = False

            print(
                "Loaded saved Section 14 snapshot instead of rerunning the API.\n"
                f"Snapshot path: {IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_RESULTS_JSON_PATH.resolve()}"
            )

        except ValueError as snapshot_validation_error:
            print(
                "Saved Section 14 snapshot is incompatible with the current code/configuration.\n"
                f"Reason: {snapshot_validation_error}\n"
                "A fresh rerun will be executed instead."
            )

    if should_run_fresh_experiments:
        if gemma_client is None:
            print(
                "Gemma client is not configured yet.\n"
                "Set GEMINI_API_KEY in the environment,\n"
                "rerun the global configuration cell,\n"
                "and then rerun this Section 14 execution cell."
            )
        else:
            improved_experimental_results = run_improved_experimental_suite(
                target_problem_records=improved_experimental_target_problem_records,
                strategy_specifications=IMPROVED_PROMPTING_STRATEGY_SPECIFICATIONS,
                zero_shot_problem_results=zero_shot_baseline_results,
                gemma_api_client=gemma_client,
                model_id=MODEL_ID,
                text_generation_backend=None,
                request_pause_seconds=IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_REQUEST_PAUSE_SECONDS,
                temperature=IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TEMPERATURE,
                top_p=IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_TOP_P,
                max_api_retries=IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_MAX_API_RETRIES,
            )

    if improved_experimental_results:
        improved_experimental_summary_dataframe = (
            build_improved_experimental_summary_dataframe(
                improved_experimental_results
            )
        )
        improved_experimental_comparison_dataframe = (
            build_improved_vs_baseline_comparison_dataframe(
                improved_experimental_results
            )
        )

        improved_experimental_saved_paths = save_improved_experimental_runs_artifacts(
            improved_experimental_results=improved_experimental_results,
            output_directory=IMPROVED_EXPERIMENTAL_RUNS_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY,
            model_id=MODEL_ID,
            target_problem_records=improved_experimental_target_problem_records,
            strategy_specifications=IMPROVED_PROMPTING_STRATEGY_SPECIFICATIONS,
        )

        best_improved_result = identify_best_improved_result(
            improved_experimental_results=improved_experimental_results,
            ranking_metric="chrf_score",
        )

        print("Improved experimental targets:")
        for target_problem_record in improved_experimental_target_problem_records:
            baseline_problem_result = get_zero_shot_problem_result_by_name(
                zero_shot_problem_results=zero_shot_baseline_results,
                problem_name=target_problem_record["name"],
            )
            print(
                f"- {target_problem_record['name']} | "
                f"baseline BLEU={baseline_problem_result['bleu_score']:.2f} | "
                f"baseline chrF={baseline_problem_result['chrf_score']:.2f} | "
                f"baseline ExactMatch={baseline_problem_result['exact_match_accuracy']:.2f}"
            )

        print("\nBaseline vs improved comparison:")
        for _, comparison_row in improved_experimental_comparison_dataframe.iterrows():
            print(
                f"- {comparison_row['problem_name']} | "
                f"{comparison_row['strategy_name']} | "
                f"BLEU {comparison_row['baseline_bleu_score']:.2f} -> "
                f"{comparison_row['improved_bleu_score']:.2f} | "
                f"chrF {comparison_row['baseline_chrf_score']:.2f} -> "
                f"{comparison_row['improved_chrf_score']:.2f} | "
                f"interpretation={comparison_row['improvement_strength']}"
            )

            f"- problem: {best_improved_result['problem_name']}\n"
            f"- strategy: {best_improved_result['strategy_name']}\n"
            f"- procedure: {best_improved_result['procedure_type']}\n"
            f"- passes: {best_improved_result['num_passes']}\n"
            f"- BLEU: {best_improved_result['bleu_score']:.2f} "
            f"(Δ {best_improved_result['bleu_score_delta_vs_baseline']:+.2f})\n"
            f"- chrF: {best_improved_result['chrf_score']:.2f} "
            f"(Δ {best_improved_result['chrf_score_delta_vs_baseline']:+.2f})\n"
            f"- Exact Match: {best_improved_result['exact_match_accuracy']:.2f}"
        )

            f"- JSON results: "
            f"{improved_experimental_saved_paths['improved_results_json_output_path'].resolve()}"
        )
        print(
            f"- CSV summary:  "

        )

        )

            improved_experimental_saved_paths["improved_report_output_path"],

Preview of the Section 14 report:

Improved experimental runs report

Target problem: Chimalapa Zoque

The improved experimental phase keeps all evaluation conditions fixed except the prompt design. For Chimalapa Zoque, the direct zero-shot baseline was BLEU 14.46, chrF 37.64, and Exact Match 0.00. The runs below therefore constitute a controlled re-evaluation of the same task, same support examples, same questions, same references, same model, and same generation settings.

Improved strategy inventory:
- recursive_bootstrapping_two_pass | procedure=multi_pass | passes=2 | role=solve from simpler correspondences to harder compositions | BLEU=8.70 (Δ -5.77) | chrF=15.87 (Δ -21.77) | ExactMatch=0.00
- explicit_rule_extraction_two_pass | procedure=multi_pass | passes=2 | role=extract a compact grammar-and-lexicon memo before answering | BLEU=4.03 (Δ -10.43) | chrF=11.74 (Δ -25.90) | ExactMatch=0.00
- generator_critic_three_pass | procedure=multi_pass | passes=3 | role=draft candidate answers, critique them, and revise into final JSON | BLEU=0.48 (Δ -13.99) | chrF=7.01 (Δ -30.63) | ExactMatch=0.00

The best improved configuration for Chimalapa Zoque is recursive_bootstrapping_two_pass, which changes the baseline from BLEU 14.46 / chrF 37.64 to BLEU 8.70 / chrF 15.87. This corresponds to a negative improvement under a strictly comparable evaluation setup.

For Chimalapa Zoque, this ranking is linguistically plausible. It is a hard ORDER problem with 18 support examples and 12 questions, so the model must stabilize local correspondences, argument structure, and sentence-order regularities across many items before producing full translations. This helps explain why recursive_bootstrapping_two_pass is the strongest configuration, even if only marginally: it encourages the model to move from simpler alignments toward larger compositions instead of forcing an early global compression of the grammar. By contrast, the explicit rule-extraction setting is more brittle on a structurally rich problem because an overcompressed memo can lock in wrong hypotheses about ordering or morphology, and the generator-critic workflow adds extra cross-pass opportunities for drift when the initial draft is already uncertain.

This section is valuable precisely because it tests whether better inference-time reasoning guidance, rather than retraining or dataset modification, is sufficient to improve Gemma’s performance on a difficult linguistic-olympiad problem.

import re

from difflib import SequenceMatcher
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

SECTION_16_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY = (
    SELECTED_SUBSET_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY / "qualitative_error_analysis"
)

SECTION_16_TARGET_PROBLEM_NAME = (
    main_target_selection_decision["primary_target_name"]
    if (
        "main_target_selection_decision" in globals()
        and isinstance(main_target_selection_decision, dict)
        and main_target_selection_decision.get("primary_target_name")
    )
    else "Chimalapa Zoque"
)

SECTION_16_PRIMARY_STRATEGY_RANKING_METRIC = "chrf_score"
SECTION_16_NEGLIGIBLE_DELTA_THRESHOLD = 0.25
# Heuristic thresholds for answer-level diagnostics

# Structural error:
# Make it easier to trigger when there is still meaningful overlap
# but the global configuration/order is wrong.
SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_TOKEN_PRECISION_THRESHOLD = 0.25
SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_TOKEN_RECALL_THRESHOLD = 0.25
SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_TOKEN_JACCARD_THRESHOLD = 0.20
SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_MIN_OVERLAP_COUNT = 2
SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_ORDER_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD = 0.80

# Semantic-role confusion:
# Decoupled from structural_error_signal and reserved for higher-overlap,
# stronger reordering cases.
SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_TOKEN_PRECISION_THRESHOLD = 0.60
SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_TOKEN_RECALL_THRESHOLD = 0.60
SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_MIN_OVERLAP_COUNT = 2
SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_MAX_LENGTH_DIFFERENCE = 1
SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_ORDER_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD = 0.55

# Morphological error:
# Make this notably stricter so it does not dominate almost every wrong answer.
SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_SENTENCE_CHRF_THRESHOLD = 35.0
SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_ALIGNED_TOKEN_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD = 0.55
SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_MIN_TOKEN_RECALL = 0.50
SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_MIN_AFFIX_RECALL = 0.50
SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_MAX_UNSUPPORTED_TOKEN_RATIO = 0.25

SECTION_16_LEXICAL_SENTENCE_CHRF_THRESHOLD = 20.0
SECTION_16_LEXICAL_TOKEN_PRECISION_THRESHOLD = 0.35
SECTION_16_LEXICAL_TOKEN_RECALL_THRESHOLD = 0.35

SECTION_16_UNSUPPORTED_TOKEN_RATIO_THRESHOLD = 0.40
SECTION_16_STRONG_DEGRADATION_CHRF_DELTA_THRESHOLD = -10.0
SECTION_16_RECURRENT_AFFIX_MIN_OCCURRENCES = 2

SECTION_16_LABEL_PREFIX_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"^\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z \-]{0,30}):\s*(.*)$"
)
SECTION_16_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"[A-Za-zÀ-ÿ0-9]+(?:['’\-][A-Za-zÀ-ÿ0-9]+)*"
)

SECTION_16_RULE_CLAIM_LINE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"\b(rule|pattern|template|means|correspond(?:s|ence)?|maps?\s+to|marks?|indicates?|therefore|thus|must|always|never)\b",
    flags=re.IGNORECASE,
)

SECTION_16_STRONG_CLAIM_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"\b(always|never|must|therefore|thus|clearly|definitely|certainly)\b",
    flags=re.IGNORECASE,
)

SECTION_16_UNCERTAINTY_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"\b(may|might|maybe|perhaps|possibly|uncertain|ambigu(?:ous|ity)|risk|guess)\b",
    flags=re.IGNORECASE,
)

SECTION_16_MAX_REASONING_EXAMPLES_PER_STRATEGY = 3

SECTION_16_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

print(f"Section 16 output directory: {SECTION_16_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY.resolve()}")
print(f"Section 16 target problem: {SECTION_16_TARGET_PROBLEM_NAME}")
print(f"Primary ranking metric: {SECTION_16_PRIMARY_STRATEGY_RANKING_METRIC}")
print(f"Negligible delta threshold: {SECTION_16_NEGLIGIBLE_DELTA_THRESHOLD}")

    f"Structural precision/recall thresholds: "

    f"{SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_TOKEN_RECALL_THRESHOLD}"
)

    f"{SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_ORDER_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD}"
)
print(
    f"Morphological chrF threshold: "
    f"{SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_SENTENCE_CHRF_THRESHOLD}"
)
print(
    f"Unsupported-token ratio threshold: "
    f"{SECTION_16_UNSUPPORTED_TOKEN_RATIO_THRESHOLD}"
)
