#CELL 16.2 - Qualitative error analysis helpers, heuristics, summaries, and artifact-saving utilities

def validate_section_16_metric_name(metric_name: str) -> None:
    supported_metric_names = {"bleu_score", "chrf_score", "exact_match_accuracy"}
    if metric_name not in supported_metric_names:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unsupported Section 16 metric name: {metric_name}. "
            f"Supported metrics: {sorted(supported_metric_names)}"
        )


def section_16_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
    summary_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    return json.loads(summary_dataframe.to_json(orient="records", force_ascii=False))


def get_section_16_problem_record_by_name(
    selected_problem_subset: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    requested_problem_name: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    requested_problem_key = canonicalize_problem_name(requested_problem_name)

    for problem_record in selected_problem_subset:
        if problem_record["name_key"] == requested_problem_key:
            return deepcopy(problem_record)

    raise ValueError(
        f"Problem '{requested_problem_name}' was not found in the selected subset."
    )


def get_section_16_problem_result_by_name(
    problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    requested_problem_name: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    requested_problem_key = canonicalize_problem_name(requested_problem_name)

    for problem_result in problem_results:
        if canonicalize_problem_name(problem_result["problem_name"]) == requested_problem_key:
            return deepcopy(problem_result)

    raise ValueError(
        f"Problem result for '{requested_problem_name}' was not found."
    )


def strip_section_16_line_initial_label(text: str) -> str:
    normalized_text = str(text).strip()
    label_match = SECTION_16_LABEL_PREFIX_PATTERN.match(normalized_text)

    if label_match:
        return label_match.group(2).strip()

    return normalized_text


def tokenize_section_16_text(text: str) -> List[str]:
    content_text = strip_section_16_line_initial_label(text)
    return SECTION_16_TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(content_text.lower())


def compute_section_16_lcs_length(
    reference_tokens: List[str],
    predicted_tokens: List[str],
) -> int:
    if not reference_tokens or not predicted_tokens:
        return 0

    num_reference_tokens = len(reference_tokens)
    num_predicted_tokens = len(predicted_tokens)

    previous_row = [0] * (num_predicted_tokens + 1)

    for reference_index in range(1, num_reference_tokens + 1):
        current_row = [0] * (num_predicted_tokens + 1)

        for predicted_index in range(1, num_predicted_tokens + 1):
            if reference_tokens[reference_index - 1] == predicted_tokens[predicted_index - 1]:
                current_row[predicted_index] = previous_row[predicted_index - 1] + 1
            else:
                current_row[predicted_index] = max(
                    current_row[predicted_index - 1],
                    previous_row[predicted_index],
                )

        previous_row = current_row

    return previous_row[-1]


def compute_section_16_token_overlap_statistics(
    reference_tokens: List[str],
    predicted_tokens: List[str],
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    reference_counter = Counter(reference_tokens)
    predicted_counter = Counter(predicted_tokens)
    overlap_counter = reference_counter & predicted_counter

    overlap_token_count = int(sum(overlap_counter.values()))
    token_precision = (
        overlap_token_count / len(predicted_tokens)
        if predicted_tokens
        else 0.0
    )
    token_recall = (
        overlap_token_count / len(reference_tokens)
        if reference_tokens
        else 0.0
    )

    reference_token_set = set(reference_tokens)
    predicted_token_set = set(predicted_tokens)
    union_token_set = reference_token_set.union(predicted_token_set)

    token_jaccard = (
        len(reference_token_set.intersection(predicted_token_set)) / len(union_token_set)
        if union_token_set
        else 1.0
    )

    return {
        "token_overlap_count": float(overlap_token_count),
        "token_precision": float(token_precision),
        "token_recall": float(token_recall),
        "token_jaccard": float(token_jaccard),
    }


def compute_section_16_order_similarity(
    reference_tokens: List[str],
    predicted_tokens: List[str],
) -> float:
    if not reference_tokens and not predicted_tokens:
        return 1.0

    normalizer = max(len(reference_tokens), len(predicted_tokens), 1)
    lcs_length = compute_section_16_lcs_length(reference_tokens, predicted_tokens)

    return float(lcs_length) / float(normalizer)


def compute_section_16_mean_aligned_token_similarity(
    reference_tokens: List[str],
    predicted_tokens: List[str],
) -> float:
    if not reference_tokens and not predicted_tokens:
        return 1.0

    if not reference_tokens or not predicted_tokens:
        return 0.0

    num_comparisons = min(len(reference_tokens), len(predicted_tokens))
    similarity_scores: List[float] = []

    for token_index in range(num_comparisons):
        similarity_scores.append(
            SequenceMatcher(
                None,
                reference_tokens[token_index],
                predicted_tokens[token_index],
            ).ratio()
        )

    if not similarity_scores:
        return 0.0

    return float(sum(similarity_scores)) / float(len(similarity_scores))


def build_section_16_problem_support_vocabulary(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
) -> set:
    support_vocabulary = set()

    for field_name in ["data", "questions"]:
        for text in problem_record[field_name]:
            support_vocabulary.update(tokenize_section_16_text(text))

    return support_vocabulary

def build_section_16_problem_affix_inventory(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    min_occurrences: int = SECTION_16_RECURRENT_AFFIX_MIN_OCCURRENCES,
) -> Dict[str, set]:
    if min_occurrences <= 0:
        raise ValueError("min_occurrences must be positive.")

    observed_tokens: List[str] = []

    # IMPORTANT FIX:
    # Use only the support examples to build the affix inventory.
    # Do NOT use the gold answers here, because that makes the diagnostic
    # too broad and leaks target-side morphology into the heuristic.
    for field_name in ["data"]:
        for text in problem_record[field_name]:
            observed_tokens.extend(tokenize_section_16_text(text))

    prefix_counter = Counter()
    suffix_counter = Counter()

    for token in observed_tokens:
        if len(token) < 4:
            continue

        for affix_length in range(2, 5):
            if len(token) <= affix_length:
                continue

            prefix_counter[token[:affix_length]] += 1
            suffix_counter[token[-affix_length:]] += 1

    recurrent_prefixes = {
        affix
        for affix, count in prefix_counter.items()
        if count >= min_occurrences
    }
    recurrent_suffixes = {
        affix
        for affix, count in suffix_counter.items()
        if count >= min_occurrences
    }

    return {
        "prefixes": recurrent_prefixes,
        "suffixes": recurrent_suffixes,
    }


def collect_section_16_matching_affixes(
    tokens: List[str],
    affix_inventory: Dict[str, set],
) -> set:
    matched_affixes = set()

    for token in tokens:
        for prefix in affix_inventory["prefixes"]:
            if token.startswith(prefix):
                matched_affixes.add(f"prefix::{prefix}")

        for suffix in affix_inventory["suffixes"]:
            if token.endswith(suffix):
                matched_affixes.add(f"suffix::{suffix}")

    return matched_affixes


def compute_section_16_affix_miss_count(
    reference_tokens: List[str],
    predicted_tokens: List[str],
    affix_inventory: Dict[str, set],
) -> int:
    reference_affixes = collect_section_16_matching_affixes(
        reference_tokens,
        affix_inventory,
    )
    predicted_affixes = collect_section_16_matching_affixes(
        predicted_tokens,
        affix_inventory,
    )

    return int(len(reference_affixes - predicted_affixes))


def compute_section_16_affix_statistics(
    reference_tokens: List[str],
    predicted_tokens: List[str],
    affix_inventory: Dict[str, set],
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    reference_affixes = collect_section_16_matching_affixes(
        reference_tokens,
        affix_inventory,
    )
    predicted_affixes = collect_section_16_matching_affixes(
        predicted_tokens,
        affix_inventory,
    )

    shared_affixes = reference_affixes.intersection(predicted_affixes)

    affix_precision = (
        len(shared_affixes) / len(predicted_affixes)
        if predicted_affixes
        else 0.0
    )
    affix_recall = (
        len(shared_affixes) / len(reference_affixes)
        if reference_affixes
        else 0.0
    )

    return {
        "reference_affix_count": int(len(reference_affixes)),
        "predicted_affix_count": int(len(predicted_affixes)),
        "shared_affix_count": int(len(shared_affixes)),
        "affix_precision": float(affix_precision),
        "affix_recall": float(affix_recall),
        "affix_miss_count": int(len(reference_affixes - predicted_affixes)),
    }

def compute_section_16_answer_features(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    reference_answer: str,
    predicted_answer: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    reference_answer = normalize_linguistic_answer_text(reference_answer)
    predicted_answer = normalize_linguistic_answer_text(predicted_answer)

    reference_tokens = tokenize_section_16_text(reference_answer)
    predicted_tokens = tokenize_section_16_text(predicted_answer)

    overlap_statistics = compute_section_16_token_overlap_statistics(
        reference_tokens=reference_tokens,
        predicted_tokens=predicted_tokens,
    )

    order_similarity = compute_section_16_order_similarity(
        reference_tokens=reference_tokens,
        predicted_tokens=predicted_tokens,
    )

    mean_aligned_token_similarity = compute_section_16_mean_aligned_token_similarity(
        reference_tokens=reference_tokens,
        predicted_tokens=predicted_tokens,
    )

    sentence_metrics = compute_sentence_level_bleu_and_chrf(
        reference_answer=reference_answer,
        predicted_answer=predicted_answer,
    )

    support_vocabulary = build_section_16_problem_support_vocabulary(problem_record)
    unsupported_predicted_tokens = [
        token
        for token in predicted_tokens
        if token not in support_vocabulary
    ]
    unsupported_token_ratio = (
        len(unsupported_predicted_tokens) / len(predicted_tokens)
        if predicted_tokens
        else 0.0
    )

    affix_inventory = build_section_16_problem_affix_inventory(problem_record)
    affix_statistics = compute_section_16_affix_statistics(
        reference_tokens=reference_tokens,
        predicted_tokens=predicted_tokens,
        affix_inventory=affix_inventory,
    )

    token_length_difference = abs(len(reference_tokens) - len(predicted_tokens))

    structural_content_overlap = bool(
        overlap_statistics["token_overlap_count"] >= SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_MIN_OVERLAP_COUNT
        and (
            overlap_statistics["token_precision"] >= SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_TOKEN_PRECISION_THRESHOLD
            or overlap_statistics["token_recall"] >= SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_TOKEN_RECALL_THRESHOLD
            or overlap_statistics["token_jaccard"] >= SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_TOKEN_JACCARD_THRESHOLD
        )
    )

    structural_error_signal = bool(
        sentence_metrics["sentence_exact_match"] == 0.0
        and len(reference_tokens) > 1
        and bool(predicted_tokens)
        and structural_content_overlap
        and order_similarity < SECTION_16_STRUCTURAL_ORDER_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD
    )

    # IMPORTANT FIX:
    # This is now independent from structural_error_signal.
    semantic_role_confusion_signal = bool(
        sentence_metrics["sentence_exact_match"] == 0.0
        and len(reference_tokens) > 1
        and bool(predicted_tokens)
        and overlap_statistics["token_overlap_count"] >= SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_MIN_OVERLAP_COUNT
        and overlap_statistics["token_precision"] >= SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_TOKEN_PRECISION_THRESHOLD
        and overlap_statistics["token_recall"] >= SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_TOKEN_RECALL_THRESHOLD
        and order_similarity < SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_ORDER_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD
        and token_length_difference <= SECTION_16_SEMANTIC_ROLE_MAX_LENGTH_DIFFERENCE
    )

    lexical_error_signal = bool(
        sentence_metrics["sentence_exact_match"] == 0.0
        and (
            (
                sentence_metrics["sentence_chrf_score"] < SECTION_16_LEXICAL_SENTENCE_CHRF_THRESHOLD
                and (
                    overlap_statistics["token_precision"] < SECTION_16_LEXICAL_TOKEN_PRECISION_THRESHOLD
                    or overlap_statistics["token_recall"] < SECTION_16_LEXICAL_TOKEN_RECALL_THRESHOLD
                )
            )
            or unsupported_token_ratio >= SECTION_16_UNSUPPORTED_TOKEN_RATIO_THRESHOLD
        )
    )

    morphological_form_similarity = bool(
        sentence_metrics["sentence_chrf_score"] >= SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_SENTENCE_CHRF_THRESHOLD
        and mean_aligned_token_similarity >= SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_ALIGNED_TOKEN_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD
        and overlap_statistics["token_recall"] >= SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_MIN_TOKEN_RECALL
    )

    morphological_affix_evidence = bool(
        affix_statistics["reference_affix_count"] > 0
        and affix_statistics["affix_miss_count"] > 0
        and affix_statistics["affix_recall"] >= SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_MIN_AFFIX_RECALL
    )

    morphological_error_signal = bool(
        sentence_metrics["sentence_exact_match"] == 0.0
        and bool(predicted_tokens)
        and unsupported_token_ratio <= SECTION_16_MORPHOLOGICAL_MAX_UNSUPPORTED_TOKEN_RATIO
        and not lexical_error_signal
        and not structural_error_signal
        and not semantic_role_confusion_signal
        and (
            morphological_affix_evidence
            or (
                morphological_form_similarity
                and token_length_difference <= 1
            )
        )
    )

    return {
        "sentence_bleu_score": float(sentence_metrics["sentence_bleu_score"]),
        "sentence_chrf_score": float(sentence_metrics["sentence_chrf_score"]),
        "sentence_exact_match": float(sentence_metrics["sentence_exact_match"]),
        "reference_token_count": int(len(reference_tokens)),
        "predicted_token_count": int(len(predicted_tokens)),
        "token_length_difference": int(token_length_difference),
        "token_overlap_count": float(overlap_statistics["token_overlap_count"]),
        "token_precision": float(overlap_statistics["token_precision"]),
        "token_recall": float(overlap_statistics["token_recall"]),
        "token_jaccard": float(overlap_statistics["token_jaccard"]),
        "order_similarity": float(order_similarity),
        "mean_aligned_token_similarity": float(mean_aligned_token_similarity),
        "unsupported_token_ratio": float(unsupported_token_ratio),
        "unsupported_token_count": int(len(unsupported_predicted_tokens)),
        "unsupported_predicted_tokens": list(unsupported_predicted_tokens),
        "reference_affix_count": int(affix_statistics["reference_affix_count"]),
        "predicted_affix_count": int(affix_statistics["predicted_affix_count"]),
        "shared_affix_count": int(affix_statistics["shared_affix_count"]),
        "affix_precision": float(affix_statistics["affix_precision"]),
        "affix_recall": float(affix_statistics["affix_recall"]),
        "affix_miss_count": int(affix_statistics["affix_miss_count"]),
        "structural_error_signal": structural_error_signal,
        "morphological_error_signal": morphological_error_signal,
        "semantic_role_confusion_signal": semantic_role_confusion_signal,
        "lexical_error_signal": lexical_error_signal,
    }


def collect_section_16_triggered_error_types(
    feature_dictionary: Dict[str, Any],
    include_hallucinated_rule: bool = False,
    hallucinated_rule_signal: bool = False,
) -> List[str]:
    triggered_error_types: List[str] = []

    if feature_dictionary.get("structural_error_signal", False):
        triggered_error_types.append("structural_error")

    if feature_dictionary.get("morphological_error_signal", False):
        triggered_error_types.append("morphological_error")

    if feature_dictionary.get("semantic_role_confusion_signal", False):
        triggered_error_types.append("semantic_role_confusion")

    if include_hallucinated_rule and hallucinated_rule_signal:
        triggered_error_types.append("hallucinated_rule")

    if feature_dictionary.get("lexical_error_signal", False):
        triggered_error_types.append("lexical_error")

    return triggered_error_types


def prefix_section_16_feature_dictionary(
    prefix: str,
    feature_dictionary: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        f"{prefix}_{feature_name}": feature_value
        for feature_name, feature_value in feature_dictionary.items()
    }


def build_section_16_answer_level_error_record(
    problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    improved_run_result: Dict[str, Any],
    question_index: int,
    question_text: str,
    reference_answer: str,
    baseline_predicted_answer: str,
    improved_predicted_answer: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    baseline_features = compute_section_16_answer_features(
        problem_record=problem_record,
        reference_answer=reference_answer,
        predicted_answer=baseline_predicted_answer,
    )
    improved_features = compute_section_16_answer_features(
        problem_record=problem_record,
        reference_answer=reference_answer,
        predicted_answer=improved_predicted_answer,
    )

    sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline = float(
        improved_features["sentence_bleu_score"] - baseline_features["sentence_bleu_score"]
    )
    sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline = float(
        improved_features["sentence_chrf_score"] - baseline_features["sentence_chrf_score"]
    )
    sentence_exact_match_delta_vs_baseline = float(
        improved_features["sentence_exact_match"] - baseline_features["sentence_exact_match"]
    )

    answer_level_comparative_outcome = (
        "improved"
        if sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline > SECTION_16_NEGLIGIBLE_DELTA_THRESHOLD
        else (
            "worse"
            if sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline < -SECTION_16_NEGLIGIBLE_DELTA_THRESHOLD
            else "tied"
        )
    )

    improved_hallucinated_rule_signal = bool(
        sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline <= SECTION_16_STRONG_DEGRADATION_CHRF_DELTA_THRESHOLD
        and (
            improved_features["unsupported_token_ratio"] >= SECTION_16_UNSUPPORTED_TOKEN_RATIO_THRESHOLD
            or (
                improved_features["token_recall"] < (baseline_features["token_recall"] - 0.20)
                and improved_features["order_similarity"] < (baseline_features["order_similarity"] - 0.15)
            )
        )
    )

    baseline_triggered_error_types = collect_section_16_triggered_error_types(
        baseline_features,
        include_hallucinated_rule=False,
    )
    improved_triggered_error_types = collect_section_16_triggered_error_types(
        improved_features,
        include_hallucinated_rule=True,
        hallucinated_rule_signal=improved_hallucinated_rule_signal,
    )

    record = {
        "problem_name": improved_run_result["problem_name"],
        "subset_order": int(improved_run_result["subset_order"]),
        "strategy_name": improved_run_result["strategy_name"],
        "strategy_version": improved_run_result["strategy_version"],
        "procedure_type": improved_run_result["procedure_type"],
        "num_passes": int(improved_run_result["num_passes"]),
        "question_index": int(question_index),
        "question_text": normalize_linguistic_answer_text(question_text),
        "reference_answer": normalize_linguistic_answer_text(reference_answer),
        "baseline_predicted_answer": normalize_linguistic_answer_text(baseline_predicted_answer),
        "improved_predicted_answer": normalize_linguistic_answer_text(improved_predicted_answer),
        "baseline_problem_bleu_score": float(improved_run_result["baseline_bleu_score"]),
        "baseline_problem_chrf_score": float(improved_run_result["baseline_chrf_score"]),
        "baseline_problem_exact_match_accuracy": float(
            improved_run_result["baseline_exact_match_accuracy"]
        ),
        "improved_problem_bleu_score": float(improved_run_result["bleu_score"]),
        "improved_problem_chrf_score": float(improved_run_result["chrf_score"]),
        "improved_problem_exact_match_accuracy": float(
            improved_run_result["exact_match_accuracy"]
        ),
        "sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline": sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline,
        "sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline": sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline,
        "sentence_exact_match_delta_vs_baseline": sentence_exact_match_delta_vs_baseline,
        "answer_level_comparative_outcome": answer_level_comparative_outcome,
        "improved_hallucinated_rule_signal": improved_hallucinated_rule_signal,
        "baseline_triggered_error_types": (
            " | ".join(baseline_triggered_error_types)
            if baseline_triggered_error_types
            else "none"
        ),
        "improved_triggered_error_types": (
            " | ".join(improved_triggered_error_types)
            if improved_triggered_error_types
            else "none"
        ),
    }

    record.update(prefix_section_16_feature_dictionary("baseline", baseline_features))
    record.update(prefix_section_16_feature_dictionary("improved", improved_features))

    return record


def build_section_16_answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe(
    selected_problem_subset: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    zero_shot_problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    target_problem_name: str = SECTION_16_TARGET_PROBLEM_NAME,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    target_problem_record = get_section_16_problem_record_by_name(
        selected_problem_subset=selected_problem_subset,
        requested_problem_name=target_problem_name,
    )
    baseline_problem_result = get_section_16_problem_result_by_name(
        problem_results=zero_shot_problem_results,
        requested_problem_name=target_problem_name,
    )

    target_improved_results = [
        deepcopy(improved_run_result)
        for improved_run_result in improved_experimental_results
        if canonicalize_problem_name(improved_run_result["problem_name"])
        == canonicalize_problem_name(target_problem_name)
    ]

    if not target_improved_results:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No improved experimental results were found for target problem '{target_problem_name}'."
        )

    answer_level_records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    baseline_questions = list(baseline_problem_result["questions"])
    baseline_reference_answers = list(baseline_problem_result["reference_answers"])
    baseline_predicted_answers = list(baseline_problem_result["predicted_answers"])

    for improved_run_result in target_improved_results:
        improved_questions = list(improved_run_result["questions"])
        improved_reference_answers = list(improved_run_result["reference_answers"])
        improved_predicted_answers = list(improved_run_result["predicted_answers"])

        if not (
            len(baseline_questions)
            == len(baseline_reference_answers)
            == len(baseline_predicted_answers)
            == len(improved_questions)
            == len(improved_reference_answers)
            == len(improved_predicted_answers)
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Question/answer length mismatch detected for problem '{target_problem_name}'."
            )

        for question_index, (
            question_text,
            reference_answer,
            baseline_predicted_answer,
            improved_predicted_answer,
        ) in enumerate(
            zip(
                baseline_questions,
                baseline_reference_answers,
                baseline_predicted_answers,
                improved_predicted_answers,
            ),
            start=1,
        ):
            answer_level_records.append(
                build_section_16_answer_level_error_record(
                    problem_record=target_problem_record,
                    improved_run_result=improved_run_result,
                    question_index=question_index,
                    question_text=question_text,
                    reference_answer=reference_answer,
                    baseline_predicted_answer=baseline_predicted_answer,
                    improved_predicted_answer=improved_predicted_answer,
                )
            )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(answer_level_records)
        .sort_values(by=["subset_order", "strategy_name", "question_index"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


def format_section_16_dominant_error_types(
    structural_count: int,
    morphological_count: int,
    semantic_role_count: int,
    hallucinated_rule_count: int,
    lexical_count: int,
) -> str:
    error_type_counts = [
        ("structural_error", structural_count),
        ("morphological_error", morphological_count),
        ("semantic_role_confusion", semantic_role_count),
        ("hallucinated_rule", hallucinated_rule_count),
        ("lexical_error", lexical_count),
    ]

    sorted_nonzero_error_types = [
        error_type_name
        for error_type_name, error_count in sorted(
            error_type_counts,
            key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0]),
        )
        if error_count > 0
    ]

    if not sorted_nonzero_error_types:
        return "none"

    return " | ".join(sorted_nonzero_error_types[:3])


def classify_section_16_hallucinated_rule_severity(
    num_hallucinated_rule_signals: int,
    num_answers: int,
    mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline: float,
    mean_improved_unsupported_token_ratio: float,
) -> str:
    if num_answers <= 0:
        return "none"

    hallucination_ratio = num_hallucinated_rule_signals / num_answers

    if (
        hallucination_ratio >= 0.50
        and mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline <= SECTION_16_STRONG_DEGRADATION_CHRF_DELTA_THRESHOLD
    ):
        return "strong"

    if (
        hallucination_ratio >= 0.25
        or mean_improved_unsupported_token_ratio >= SECTION_16_UNSUPPORTED_TOKEN_RATIO_THRESHOLD
    ):
        return "moderate"

    if num_hallucinated_rule_signals > 0:
        return "weak"

    return "none"

def safe_section_16_rate(count: int, total: int) -> float:
    if total <= 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(count) / float(total)


def format_section_16_count_and_rate(count: int, total: int) -> str:
    return f"{count}/{total} ({safe_section_16_rate(count, total):.2f} per answer)"


def split_section_16_stage_text_into_lines(text: str) -> List[str]:
    return [line.strip() for line in str(text).splitlines() if line.strip()]


def section_16_line_has_support_token(line: str, support_vocabulary: set) -> bool:
    line_tokens = tokenize_section_16_text(line)
    return bool(set(line_tokens).intersection(support_vocabulary))

def build_section_16_strategy_error_summary_dataframe(
    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build the Section 16 strategy summary from an empty answer-level dataframe."
        )

    summary_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    grouped_dataframe = answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe.groupby(
        ["problem_name", "strategy_name", "strategy_version", "procedure_type", "num_passes"],
        sort=False,
    )

    for (
        problem_name,
        strategy_name,
        strategy_version,
        procedure_type,
        num_passes,
    ), strategy_dataframe in grouped_dataframe:
        num_answers = int(len(strategy_dataframe))
        num_answers_improved_vs_baseline = int(
            (strategy_dataframe["answer_level_comparative_outcome"] == "improved").sum()
        )
        num_answers_worse_vs_baseline = int(
            (strategy_dataframe["answer_level_comparative_outcome"] == "worse").sum()
        )
        num_answers_tied_vs_baseline = int(
            (strategy_dataframe["answer_level_comparative_outcome"] == "tied").sum()
        )

        num_baseline_structural_error_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["baseline_structural_error_signal"].sum()
        )
        num_baseline_morphological_error_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["baseline_morphological_error_signal"].sum()
        )
        num_baseline_semantic_role_confusion_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["baseline_semantic_role_confusion_signal"].sum()
        )
        num_baseline_lexical_error_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["baseline_lexical_error_signal"].sum()
        )

        num_structural_error_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["improved_structural_error_signal"].sum()
        )
        num_morphological_error_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["improved_morphological_error_signal"].sum()
        )
        num_semantic_role_confusion_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["improved_semantic_role_confusion_signal"].sum()
        )
        num_hallucinated_rule_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["improved_hallucinated_rule_signal"].sum()
        )
        num_lexical_error_signals = int(
            strategy_dataframe["improved_lexical_error_signal"].sum()
        )

        total_improved_error_signals = int(
            num_structural_error_signals
            + num_morphological_error_signals
            + num_semantic_role_confusion_signals
            + num_hallucinated_rule_signals
            + num_lexical_error_signals
        )

        mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline = float(
            strategy_dataframe["sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline"].mean()
        )
        mean_improved_unsupported_token_ratio = float(
            strategy_dataframe["improved_unsupported_token_ratio"].mean()
        )

        incomplete_decomposition_signal = bool(
            num_answers_improved_vs_baseline > 0
            and num_answers_worse_vs_baseline > num_answers_improved_vs_baseline
        )

        summary_rows.append(
            {
                "problem_name": problem_name,
                "strategy_name": strategy_name,
                "strategy_version": strategy_version,
                "procedure_type": procedure_type,
                "num_passes": int(num_passes),
                "num_answers": num_answers,
                "baseline_problem_bleu_score": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["baseline_problem_bleu_score"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "baseline_problem_chrf_score": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["baseline_problem_chrf_score"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "baseline_problem_exact_match_accuracy": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["baseline_problem_exact_match_accuracy"].iloc[0]
                ),
                               "baseline_structural_error_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_baseline_structural_error_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "baseline_morphological_error_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_baseline_morphological_error_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "baseline_semantic_role_confusion_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_baseline_semantic_role_confusion_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "baseline_lexical_error_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_baseline_lexical_error_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "structural_error_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_structural_error_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "morphological_error_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_morphological_error_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "semantic_role_confusion_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_semantic_role_confusion_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "hallucinated_rule_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_hallucinated_rule_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "lexical_error_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_lexical_error_signals, num_answers
                ),
                "improved_problem_bleu_score": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_problem_bleu_score"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "improved_problem_chrf_score": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_problem_chrf_score"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "improved_problem_exact_match_accuracy": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_problem_exact_match_accuracy"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "problem_bleu_delta_vs_baseline": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_problem_bleu_score"].iloc[0]
                    - strategy_dataframe["baseline_problem_bleu_score"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "problem_chrf_delta_vs_baseline": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_problem_chrf_score"].iloc[0]
                    - strategy_dataframe["baseline_problem_chrf_score"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "problem_exact_match_delta_vs_baseline": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_problem_exact_match_accuracy"].iloc[0]
                    - strategy_dataframe["baseline_problem_exact_match_accuracy"].iloc[0]
                ),
                "num_answers_improved_vs_baseline": num_answers_improved_vs_baseline,
                "num_answers_worse_vs_baseline": num_answers_worse_vs_baseline,
                "num_answers_tied_vs_baseline": num_answers_tied_vs_baseline,
                "num_baseline_structural_error_signals": num_baseline_structural_error_signals,
                "num_baseline_morphological_error_signals": num_baseline_morphological_error_signals,
                "num_baseline_semantic_role_confusion_signals": num_baseline_semantic_role_confusion_signals,
                "num_baseline_lexical_error_signals": num_baseline_lexical_error_signals,
                "num_structural_error_signals": num_structural_error_signals,
                "num_morphological_error_signals": num_morphological_error_signals,
                "num_semantic_role_confusion_signals": num_semantic_role_confusion_signals,
                "num_hallucinated_rule_signals": num_hallucinated_rule_signals,
                "num_lexical_error_signals": num_lexical_error_signals,
                "total_improved_error_signals": total_improved_error_signals,
                "mean_improved_order_similarity": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_order_similarity"].mean()
                ),
                "mean_improved_unsupported_token_ratio": mean_improved_unsupported_token_ratio,
                "mean_improved_affix_miss_count": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["improved_affix_miss_count"].mean()
                ),
                "mean_sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline": float(
                    strategy_dataframe["sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline"].mean()
                ),
                "mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline": mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline,
                "incomplete_decomposition_signal": incomplete_decomposition_signal,
                "hallucinated_rule_severity": classify_section_16_hallucinated_rule_severity(
                    num_hallucinated_rule_signals=num_hallucinated_rule_signals,
                    num_answers=num_answers,
                    mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline=mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline,
                    mean_improved_unsupported_token_ratio=mean_improved_unsupported_token_ratio,
                ),
                "dominant_error_types": format_section_16_dominant_error_types(
                    structural_count=num_structural_error_signals,
                    morphological_count=num_morphological_error_signals,
                    semantic_role_count=num_semantic_role_confusion_signals,
                    hallucinated_rule_count=num_hallucinated_rule_signals,
                    lexical_count=num_lexical_error_signals,
                ),
            }
        )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(summary_rows)
        .sort_values(
            by=["improved_problem_chrf_score", "improved_problem_bleu_score"],
            ascending=[False, False],
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

def build_section_16_overall_error_profile_dataframe(
    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build the Section 16 overall error profile from an empty dataframe."
        )

    baseline_unique_dataframe = (
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe
        .sort_values(by=["problem_name", "question_index", "strategy_name"])
        .drop_duplicates(subset=["problem_name", "question_index"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    def build_profile_row(
        evaluation_scope: str,
        dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
        signal_prefix: str,
        include_hallucinated_rule: bool,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        num_answer_records = int(len(dataframe))

        structural_count = int(dataframe[f"{signal_prefix}_structural_error_signal"].sum())
        morphological_count = int(dataframe[f"{signal_prefix}_morphological_error_signal"].sum())
        semantic_role_count = int(dataframe[f"{signal_prefix}_semantic_role_confusion_signal"].sum())
        lexical_count = int(dataframe[f"{signal_prefix}_lexical_error_signal"].sum())

        row = {
            "evaluation_scope": evaluation_scope,
            "num_answer_records": num_answer_records,
            "num_structural_error_signals": structural_count,
            "num_morphological_error_signals": morphological_count,
            "num_semantic_role_confusion_signals": semantic_role_count,
            "num_lexical_error_signals": lexical_count,
            "structural_error_rate_per_answer": safe_section_16_rate(
                structural_count, num_answer_records
            ),
            "morphological_error_rate_per_answer": safe_section_16_rate(
                morphological_count, num_answer_records
            ),
            "semantic_role_confusion_rate_per_answer": safe_section_16_rate(
                semantic_role_count, num_answer_records
            ),
            "lexical_error_rate_per_answer": safe_section_16_rate(
                lexical_count, num_answer_records
            ),
            "mean_sentence_chrf_score": float(
                dataframe[f"{signal_prefix}_sentence_chrf_score"].mean()
            ),
            "mean_order_similarity": float(
                dataframe[f"{signal_prefix}_order_similarity"].mean()
            ),
        }

        if include_hallucinated_rule:
            hallucinated_count = int(dataframe["improved_hallucinated_rule_signal"].sum())
            row["num_hallucinated_rule_signals"] = hallucinated_count
            row["hallucinated_rule_rate_per_answer"] = safe_section_16_rate(
                hallucinated_count, num_answer_records
            )
        else:
            row["num_hallucinated_rule_signals"] = 0
            row["hallucinated_rule_rate_per_answer"] = 0.0

        return row

    profile_rows = [
        build_profile_row(
            evaluation_scope="baseline_target_problem_unique_answers",
            dataframe=baseline_unique_dataframe,
            signal_prefix="baseline",
            include_hallucinated_rule=False,
        ),
        build_profile_row(
            evaluation_scope="improved_all_strategies_aggregate_answers",
            dataframe=answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe,
            signal_prefix="improved",
            include_hallucinated_rule=True,
        ),
    ]

    return pd.DataFrame(profile_rows)

def build_section_16_illustrative_error_examples_payload(
    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    max_examples_per_error_type: int = 3,
) -> Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    if answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build illustrative examples from an empty answer-level dataframe."
        )

    error_type_column_mapping = {
        "structural_error": "improved_structural_error_signal",
        "morphological_error": "improved_morphological_error_signal",
        "semantic_role_confusion": "improved_semantic_role_confusion_signal",
        "hallucinated_rule": "improved_hallucinated_rule_signal",
        "lexical_error": "improved_lexical_error_signal",
    }

    illustrative_examples_payload: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}

    for error_type_name, signal_column_name in error_type_column_mapping.items():
        filtered_dataframe = answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe[
            answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe[signal_column_name]
        ].copy()

        if filtered_dataframe.empty:
            illustrative_examples_payload[error_type_name] = []
            continue

        filtered_dataframe = filtered_dataframe.sort_values(
            by=["sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline", "sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline"],
            ascending=[True, True],
        ).head(max_examples_per_error_type)

        illustrative_examples_payload[error_type_name] = [
            {
                "problem_name": row["problem_name"],
                "strategy_name": row["strategy_name"],
                "question_index": int(row["question_index"]),
                "question_text": row["question_text"],
                "reference_answer": row["reference_answer"],
                "baseline_predicted_answer": row["baseline_predicted_answer"],
                "improved_predicted_answer": row["improved_predicted_answer"],
                "sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline": float(
                    row["sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline"]
                ),
                "sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline": float(
                    row["sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline"]
                ),
                "improved_triggered_error_types": row["improved_triggered_error_types"],
            }
            for _, row in filtered_dataframe.iterrows()
        ]

    return illustrative_examples_payload


def identify_section_16_best_strategy_row(
    strategy_error_summary_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    ranking_metric: str = SECTION_16_PRIMARY_STRATEGY_RANKING_METRIC,
) -> pd.Series:
    validate_section_16_metric_name(ranking_metric)

    if strategy_error_summary_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot identify the best Section 16 strategy from an empty summary dataframe."
        )

    sort_column_name = (
        "improved_problem_chrf_score"
        if ranking_metric == "chrf_score"
        else (
            "improved_problem_bleu_score"
            if ranking_metric == "bleu_score"
            else "improved_problem_exact_match_accuracy"
        )
    )

    return (
        strategy_error_summary_dataframe
        .sort_values(
            by=[sort_column_name, "improved_problem_bleu_score"],
            ascending=[False, False],
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
        .iloc[0]
    )


def format_section_16_question_indices(question_indices: List[int]) -> str:
    if not question_indices:
        return "no especially strong answer-level instances"

    return ", ".join(str(question_index) for question_index in question_indices)


def get_section_16_top_question_indices_for_error_type(
    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    strategy_name: str,
    error_signal_column_name: str,
    max_examples: int = 3,
) -> List[int]:
    filtered_dataframe = answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe[
        (answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe["strategy_name"] == strategy_name)
        & (answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe[error_signal_column_name])
    ].copy()

    if filtered_dataframe.empty:
        return []

    filtered_dataframe = filtered_dataframe.sort_values(
        by=["sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline", "sentence_bleu_delta_vs_baseline"],
        ascending=[True, True],
    ).head(max_examples)

    return [int(question_index) for question_index in filtered_dataframe["question_index"].tolist()]


def maybe_get_section_16_problem_result(
    problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    problem_name: str,
) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        return get_section_16_problem_result_by_name(problem_results, problem_name)
    except ValueError:
        return None

def build_section_16_intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe(
    selected_problem_subset: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    target_problem_name: str = SECTION_16_TARGET_PROBLEM_NAME,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    target_problem_record = get_section_16_problem_record_by_name(
        selected_problem_subset=selected_problem_subset,
        requested_problem_name=target_problem_name,
    )

    support_vocabulary = build_section_16_problem_support_vocabulary(target_problem_record)

    target_improved_results = [
        deepcopy(improved_run_result)
        for improved_run_result in improved_experimental_results
        if canonicalize_problem_name(improved_run_result["problem_name"])
        == canonicalize_problem_name(target_problem_name)
    ]

    if not target_improved_results:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No improved experimental results were found for target problem '{target_problem_name}'."
        )

    diagnostic_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for improved_run_result in target_improved_results:
        non_final_stage_records = [
            deepcopy(stage_record)
            for stage_record in improved_run_result.get("stage_records", [])
            if stage_record.get("stage_name") != "final_json_answers"
        ]

        num_rule_claim_lines = 0
        num_supported_rule_claim_lines = 0
        num_unsupported_rule_claim_lines = 0
        num_strong_rule_claim_lines = 0
        num_uncertainty_marker_lines = 0

        unsupported_rule_claim_examples: List[str] = []

        for stage_record in non_final_stage_records:
            for line in split_section_16_stage_text_into_lines(
                stage_record.get("raw_model_response", "")
            ):
                if SECTION_16_UNCERTAINTY_PATTERN.search(line):
                    num_uncertainty_marker_lines += 1

                if SECTION_16_RULE_CLAIM_LINE_PATTERN.search(line):
                    num_rule_claim_lines += 1

                    if SECTION_16_STRONG_CLAIM_PATTERN.search(line):
                        num_strong_rule_claim_lines += 1

                    if section_16_line_has_support_token(line, support_vocabulary):
                        num_supported_rule_claim_lines += 1
                    else:
                        num_unsupported_rule_claim_lines += 1
                        if len(unsupported_rule_claim_examples) < SECTION_16_MAX_REASONING_EXAMPLES_PER_STRATEGY:
                            unsupported_rule_claim_examples.append(line[:180])

        diagnostic_rows.append(
            {
                "problem_name": improved_run_result["problem_name"],
                "strategy_name": improved_run_result["strategy_name"],
                "strategy_version": improved_run_result["strategy_version"],
                "procedure_type": improved_run_result["procedure_type"],
                "num_passes": int(improved_run_result["num_passes"]),
                "num_non_final_stages": int(len(non_final_stage_records)),
                "improved_problem_bleu_score": float(improved_run_result["bleu_score"]),
                "improved_problem_chrf_score": float(improved_run_result["chrf_score"]),
                "num_rule_claim_lines": int(num_rule_claim_lines),
                "num_supported_rule_claim_lines": int(num_supported_rule_claim_lines),
                "num_unsupported_rule_claim_lines": int(num_unsupported_rule_claim_lines),
                "num_strong_rule_claim_lines": int(num_strong_rule_claim_lines),
                "num_uncertainty_marker_lines": int(num_uncertainty_marker_lines),
                "unsupported_rule_claim_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_unsupported_rule_claim_lines, num_rule_claim_lines
                ),
                "strong_rule_claim_rate": safe_section_16_rate(
                    num_strong_rule_claim_lines, num_rule_claim_lines
                ),
                "unsupported_rule_claim_examples": " || ".join(unsupported_rule_claim_examples)
                if unsupported_rule_claim_examples
                else "none",
            }
        )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(diagnostic_rows)
        .sort_values(
            by=["improved_problem_chrf_score", "improved_problem_bleu_score"],
            ascending=[False, False],
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )
def build_section_16_report_text(
    target_problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    zero_shot_problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    strategy_error_summary_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
    contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
) -> str:
    if answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build the Section 16 report from an empty answer-level dataframe."
        )

    if strategy_error_summary_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot build the Section 16 report from an empty strategy summary dataframe."
        )

    target_problem_name = target_problem_record["name"]
    num_questions = int(target_problem_record["num_questions"])

    baseline_problem_result = get_section_16_problem_result_by_name(
        problem_results=zero_shot_problem_results,
        requested_problem_name=target_problem_name,
    )

    best_strategy_row = identify_section_16_best_strategy_row(
        strategy_error_summary_dataframe=strategy_error_summary_dataframe,
        ranking_metric=SECTION_16_PRIMARY_STRATEGY_RANKING_METRIC,
    )
    best_strategy_name = str(best_strategy_row["strategy_name"])

    overall_profile_dataframe = build_section_16_overall_error_profile_dataframe(
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe
    )

    baseline_profile_row = overall_profile_dataframe[
        overall_profile_dataframe["evaluation_scope"] == "baseline_target_problem_unique_answers"
    ].iloc[0]

    improved_profile_row = overall_profile_dataframe[
        overall_profile_dataframe["evaluation_scope"] == "improved_all_strategies_aggregate_answers"
    ].iloc[0]

    best_reasoning_row = None
    if (
        intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe is not None
        and not intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe.empty
    ):
        reasoning_matches = intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe[
            intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe["strategy_name"] == best_strategy_name
        ]
        if not reasoning_matches.empty:
            best_reasoning_row = reasoning_matches.iloc[0]

    improved_total_answers = int(improved_profile_row["num_answer_records"])
    baseline_total_answers = int(baseline_profile_row["num_answer_records"])

    aggregate_error_count_pairs = [
        ("lexical_error", int(improved_profile_row["num_lexical_error_signals"])),
        ("hallucinated_rule", int(improved_profile_row["num_hallucinated_rule_signals"])),
        ("structural_error", int(improved_profile_row["num_structural_error_signals"])),
        ("morphological_error", int(improved_profile_row["num_morphological_error_signals"])),
        ("semantic_role_confusion", int(improved_profile_row["num_semantic_role_confusion_signals"])),
    ]
    aggregate_error_count_pairs = [
        (error_name, error_count)
        for error_name, error_count in sorted(
            aggregate_error_count_pairs,
            key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0]),
        )
        if error_count > 0
    ]

    aggregate_error_profile_text = ", ".join(
        f"{error_name} {format_section_16_count_and_rate(error_count, improved_total_answers)}"
        for error_name, error_count in aggregate_error_count_pairs
    )
    if not aggregate_error_profile_text:
        aggregate_error_profile_text = "none"

    best_strategy_error_profile_text = ", ".join(
        [
            f"lexical_error {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(best_strategy_row['num_lexical_error_signals']), num_questions)}",
            f"hallucinated_rule {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(best_strategy_row['num_hallucinated_rule_signals']), num_questions)}",
            f"structural_error {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(best_strategy_row['num_structural_error_signals']), num_questions)}",
            f"morphological_error {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(best_strategy_row['num_morphological_error_signals']), num_questions)}",
            f"semantic_role_confusion {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(best_strategy_row['num_semantic_role_confusion_signals']), num_questions)}",
        ]
    )

    baseline_profile_text = ", ".join(
        [
            f"structural_error {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(baseline_profile_row['num_structural_error_signals']), baseline_total_answers)}",
            f"morphological_error {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(baseline_profile_row['num_morphological_error_signals']), baseline_total_answers)}",
            f"lexical_error {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(baseline_profile_row['num_lexical_error_signals']), baseline_total_answers)}",
        ]
    )

    structural_question_indices = get_section_16_top_question_indices_for_error_type(
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe=answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe,
        strategy_name=best_strategy_name,
        error_signal_column_name="improved_structural_error_signal",
        max_examples=3,
    )
    morphological_question_indices = get_section_16_top_question_indices_for_error_type(
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe=answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe,
        strategy_name=best_strategy_name,
        error_signal_column_name="improved_morphological_error_signal",
        max_examples=3,
    )
    semantic_role_question_indices = get_section_16_top_question_indices_for_error_type(
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe=answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe,
        strategy_name=best_strategy_name,
        error_signal_column_name="improved_semantic_role_confusion_signal",
        max_examples=3,
    )
    lexical_question_indices = get_section_16_top_question_indices_for_error_type(
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe=answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe,
        strategy_name=best_strategy_name,
        error_signal_column_name="improved_lexical_error_signal",
        max_examples=3,
    )

    easier_order_results: List[str] = []
    engenni_result = maybe_get_section_16_problem_result(zero_shot_problem_results, "Engenni 5")
    rapa_nui_result = maybe_get_section_16_problem_result(zero_shot_problem_results, "Rapa Nui 5")

    if engenni_result is not None:
        easier_order_results.append(
            f"Engenni 5 (BLEU {engenni_result['bleu_score']:.2f}, chrF {engenni_result['chrf_score']:.2f})"
        )
    if rapa_nui_result is not None:
        easier_order_results.append(
            f"Rapa Nui 5 (BLEU {rapa_nui_result['bleu_score']:.2f}, chrF {rapa_nui_result['chrf_score']:.2f})"
        )

    contamination_interpretation_text = ""
    if (
        contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe is not None
        and not contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe.empty
    ):
        stable_rows = contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe[
            contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe["interpretation"]
            == "stable_consistent_with_structure_based_reasoning"
        ].copy()

        if not stable_rows.empty:
            stable_fragments = [
                f"{row['source_problem_name']} (chrF retention {float(row['chrf_retention_ratio']):.4f})"
                for _, row in stable_rows.iterrows()
            ]
            contamination_interpretation_text = (
                " The contamination check also remained stable for "
                + ", ".join(stable_fragments)
                + ", which is more compatible with genuine structure-based reasoning than with simple dependence on original lexical surface forms."
            )

    report_paragraphs: List[str] = []

    report_paragraphs.append("Qualitative error analysis report")

    report_paragraphs.append(
        (
            f"For the main target problem, {target_problem_name}, the direct zero-shot baseline "
            f"already performs weakly (BLEU {baseline_problem_result['bleu_score']:.2f}, chrF "
            f"{baseline_problem_result['chrf_score']:.2f}, Exact Match "
            f"{baseline_problem_result['exact_match_accuracy']:.2f}), and every multi-pass "
            f"prompting strategy degrades that baseline. The least harmful strategy is "
            f"{best_strategy_name}, but even that configuration falls to BLEU "
            f"{best_strategy_row['improved_problem_bleu_score']:.2f} and chrF "
            f"{best_strategy_row['improved_problem_chrf_score']:.2f}. The goal of this section "
            f"is therefore diagnostic: to identify which error categories actually dominate the "
            f"failed improved runs, and to ensure that the narrative follows the computed evidence."
        )
    )

    report_paragraphs.append(
        (
            f"The denominator-normalized comparison is essential here. The baseline profile is computed "
            f"over {baseline_total_answers} unique target answers, whereas the improved aggregate profile "
            f"is computed over {improved_total_answers} answer records because it pools the three tested "
            f"strategies. The baseline target profile is mixed: {baseline_profile_text}. By contrast, the "
            f"aggregate improved-run profile is dominated by {aggregate_error_profile_text}. This means "
            f"the improved runs are not mainly characterized by morphology or semantic-role confusion under "
            f"the current heuristics; instead, they are dominated by lexical failure, hallucinated-rule "
            f"behaviour, and a smaller amount of structural error."
        )
    )

    report_paragraphs.append(
        (
            f"For the strongest strategy under the current ranking, {best_strategy_name}, the answer-level "
            f"profile is: {best_strategy_error_profile_text}. This strategy still improves only "
            f"{int(best_strategy_row['num_answers_improved_vs_baseline'])} of {num_questions} answers while "
            f"worsening {int(best_strategy_row['num_answers_worse_vs_baseline'])}, so even the best multi-pass "
            f"configuration remains clearly below the direct zero-shot baseline."
        )
    )

    structural_count_best = int(best_strategy_row["num_structural_error_signals"])
    if structural_count_best > 0:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                f"Structural error is therefore present, but it should be described as secondary rather than "
                f"dominant. Under {best_strategy_name}, structure-related signals appear in "
                f"{format_section_16_count_and_rate(structural_count_best, num_questions)}. This supports the "
                f"claim that some answers recover part of the right material but assemble it in the wrong order "
                f"or global configuration. The strongest structure-related instances under {best_strategy_name} "
                f"appear in question(s) {format_section_16_question_indices(structural_question_indices)}."
            )
        )
    else:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                "Structural error should not be emphasized as a major category in the improved runs under the "
                "current calibrated heuristics."
            )
        )

    improved_morphological_total = int(improved_profile_row["num_morphological_error_signals"])
    morphological_count_best = int(best_strategy_row["num_morphological_error_signals"])
    baseline_morphological_total = int(baseline_profile_row["num_morphological_error_signals"])

    if improved_morphological_total == 0 and morphological_count_best == 0:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                f"Morphological error should not be presented as a dominant improved-run category. The improved "
                f"aggregate profile contains {format_section_16_count_and_rate(improved_morphological_total, improved_total_answers)}, "
                f"and {best_strategy_name} contains {format_section_16_count_and_rate(morphological_count_best, num_questions)}. "
                f"The only place where morphology remains visible is the baseline target profile, where it appears in "
                f"{format_section_16_count_and_rate(baseline_morphological_total, baseline_total_answers)}. Accordingly, the final "
                f"report should treat morphology as a baseline-side or weak residual signal here, not as one of the main improved-run "
                f"dominant classes."
            )
        )
    else:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                f"Morphological error is present under the improved runs in "
                f"{format_section_16_count_and_rate(improved_morphological_total, improved_total_answers)} overall and "
                f"{format_section_16_count_and_rate(morphological_count_best, num_questions)} for {best_strategy_name}. "
                f"The strongest morphology-related instances under {best_strategy_name} appear in question(s) "
                f"{format_section_16_question_indices(morphological_question_indices)}."
            )
        )

    lexical_count_best = int(best_strategy_row["num_lexical_error_signals"])
    report_paragraphs.append(
        (
            f"Lexical error is the clearest dominant class in the improved runs. Under {best_strategy_name}, lexical "
            f"signals appear in {format_section_16_count_and_rate(lexical_count_best, num_questions)}, and across all "
            f"improved strategies they appear in {format_section_16_count_and_rate(int(improved_profile_row['num_lexical_error_signals']), improved_total_answers)}. "
            f"This is why the section should not claim that the bottleneck is mainly non-lexical. In the present run, "
            f"lexical failure is central, especially in question(s) "
            f"{format_section_16_question_indices(lexical_question_indices)}."
        )
    )

    semantic_role_count_best = int(best_strategy_row["num_semantic_role_confusion_signals"])
    improved_semantic_total = int(improved_profile_row["num_semantic_role_confusion_signals"])

    if improved_semantic_total == 0 and semantic_role_count_best == 0:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                f"Semantic-role confusion is not robustly supported as an independent dominant category in this run. "
                f"The improved aggregate profile contains {format_section_16_count_and_rate(improved_semantic_total, improved_total_answers)}, "
                f"and {best_strategy_name} contains {format_section_16_count_and_rate(semantic_role_count_best, num_questions)}. "
                f"Accordingly, the final interpretation should not describe semantic-role confusion as recurring unless a future rerun "
                f"actually raises these counts."
            )
        )
    else:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                f"Semantic-role confusion is independently detected in "
                f"{format_section_16_count_and_rate(improved_semantic_total, improved_total_answers)} overall and "
                f"{format_section_16_count_and_rate(semantic_role_count_best, num_questions)} for {best_strategy_name}. "
                f"The strongest instances appear in question(s) "
                f"{format_section_16_question_indices(semantic_role_question_indices)}."
            )
        )

    if best_reasoning_row is not None:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                f"Hallucinated-rule behaviour is now supported not only by output degradation but also by direct inspection "
                f"of the intermediate reasoning texts. For {best_strategy_name}, the non-final stages contain "
                f"{int(best_reasoning_row['num_rule_claim_lines'])} rule-claim lines, "
                f"{int(best_reasoning_row['num_unsupported_rule_claim_lines'])} of which have no explicit support-token grounding, "
                f"and {int(best_reasoning_row['num_strong_rule_claim_lines'])} strong-claim lines. This does not prove hallucination "
                f"in a strict causal sense, but it does provide memo-level evidence that unsupported generalizations are appearing "
                f"before the final answers are produced."
            )
        )
    else:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                "Hallucinated-rule behaviour is still supported by the output-side heuristics, but if the intermediate-stage "
                "diagnostics are unavailable then the wording should remain cautious and avoid overclaiming."
            )
        )

    report_paragraphs.append(
        (
            f"Incomplete decomposition also remains strongly supported by the answer-level comparison. The best strategy, "
            f"{best_strategy_name}, improves {int(best_strategy_row['num_answers_improved_vs_baseline'])} answers but worsens "
            f"{int(best_strategy_row['num_answers_worse_vs_baseline'])}, with mean sentence-level chrF delta "
            f"{best_strategy_row['mean_sentence_chrf_delta_vs_baseline']:+.2f}. More elaborate strategies become progressively "
            f"worse, which is consistent with the idea that extra reasoning passes amplify drift instead of stabilizing the solution."
        )
    )

    if easier_order_results:
        report_paragraphs.append(
            (
                f"At a broader level, this pattern also helps explain why Gemma behaves much better on easier ORDER cases than on "
                f"a structurally richer hard case such as {target_problem_name}. In the baseline, the easier ORDER controls performed "
                f"much better, especially {' and '.join(easier_order_results)}."
                f"{contamination_interpretation_text} This is compatible with the idea that the model can exploit genuine structural "
                f"regularities when the system is simpler, but struggles once many interacting regularities must be tracked simultaneously."
            )
        )

    report_paragraphs.append(
        (
            f"The safest final interpretation is therefore fully data-driven: in the current run, the dominant improved-run pattern is "
            f"lexical failure, hallucinated-rule behaviour, and some structural error. Morphological instability and semantic-role confusion "
            f"should not be presented as dominant improved-run categories here, because the calibrated counts do not support that claim. "
            f"If a future rerun changes the counts, the narrative should change with them."
        )
    )

    return "\n\n".join(report_paragraphs)

def build_section_16_payload(
    target_problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    zero_shot_problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    strategy_error_summary_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    overall_error_profile_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    illustrative_error_examples_payload: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]],
    intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
    contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "analysis_name": "qualitative_error_analysis",
        "target_problem_name": target_problem_record["name"],
        "target_problem_record": target_problem_record,
        "baseline_problem_result": get_section_16_problem_result_by_name(
            problem_results=zero_shot_problem_results,
            requested_problem_name=target_problem_record["name"],
        ),
        "improved_problem_results": [
            deepcopy(improved_run_result)
            for improved_run_result in improved_experimental_results
            if canonicalize_problem_name(improved_run_result["problem_name"])
            == canonicalize_problem_name(target_problem_record["name"])
        ],
        "answer_level_error_analysis_records": section_16_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
            answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe
        ),
        "strategy_error_summary_records": section_16_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
            strategy_error_summary_dataframe
        ),
        "overall_error_profile_records": section_16_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
            overall_error_profile_dataframe
        ),
        "illustrative_error_examples": illustrative_error_examples_payload,
        "intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_records": (
            section_16_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
                intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe
            )
            if (
                intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe is not None
                and not intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe.empty
            )
            else []
        ),
    }

    if contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe is not None and not contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe.empty:
        payload["contamination_metric_comparison_records"] = (
            section_16_dataframe_to_json_serializable_records(
                contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe
            )
        )

    return payload

def save_section_16_qualitative_error_analysis_artifacts(
    target_problem_record: Dict[str, Any],
    zero_shot_problem_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    improved_experimental_results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    strategy_error_summary_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    overall_error_profile_dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    illustrative_error_examples_payload: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]],
    intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe: Optional[pd.DataFrame],
    output_directory: Path,
    contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Path]:
    if answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot save Section 16 artifacts because the answer-level dataframe is empty."
        )

    if strategy_error_summary_dataframe.empty:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cannot save Section 16 artifacts because the strategy-summary dataframe is empty."
        )

    output_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    section_16_report_text = build_section_16_report_text(
        target_problem_record=section_16_target_problem_record,
        zero_shot_problem_results=zero_shot_baseline_results,
        improved_experimental_results=improved_experimental_results,
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe=section_16_answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe,
        strategy_error_summary_dataframe=section_16_strategy_error_summary_dataframe,
        intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe=section_16_intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe,
        contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe=optional_section_16_contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe,
    )

    section_16_payload = build_section_16_payload(
        target_problem_record=target_problem_record,
        zero_shot_problem_results=zero_shot_problem_results,
        improved_experimental_results=improved_experimental_results,
        answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe=answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe,
        strategy_error_summary_dataframe=strategy_error_summary_dataframe,
        overall_error_profile_dataframe=overall_error_profile_dataframe,
        illustrative_error_examples_payload=illustrative_error_examples_payload,
        intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe=intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe,
        contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe=contamination_metric_comparison_dataframe,
    )

    section_16_json_output_path = (
        output_directory / "section_16_qualitative_error_analysis.json"
    )
    section_16_answer_level_csv_output_path = (
        output_directory / "section_16_answer_level_error_analysis.csv"
    )
    section_16_strategy_summary_csv_output_path = (
        output_directory / "section_16_strategy_error_summary.csv"
    )
    section_16_overall_profile_csv_output_path = (
        output_directory / "section_16_overall_error_profile.csv"
    )
    section_16_intermediate_reasoning_csv_output_path = (
        output_directory / "section_16_intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics.csv"
    )
    section_16_examples_json_output_path = (
        output_directory / "section_16_illustrative_error_examples.json"
    )
    section_16_report_output_path = (
        output_directory / "section_16_qualitative_error_analysis_report.txt"
    )

    save_json_file(section_16_payload, section_16_json_output_path)

    answer_level_error_analysis_dataframe.to_csv(
        section_16_answer_level_csv_output_path,
        index=False,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    strategy_error_summary_dataframe.to_csv(
        section_16_strategy_summary_csv_output_path,
        index=False,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    overall_error_profile_dataframe.to_csv(
        section_16_overall_profile_csv_output_path,
        index=False,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    if (
        intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe is not None
        and not intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe.empty
    ):
        intermediate_reasoning_diagnostics_dataframe.to_csv(
            section_16_intermediate_reasoning_csv_output_path,
            index=False,
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

    save_json_file(
        {"illustrative_error_examples": illustrative_error_examples_payload},
        section_16_examples_json_output_path,
    )

    with open(section_16_report_output_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as report_file:
        report_file.write(section_16_report_text)

    return {
        "section_16_json_output_path": section_16_json_output_path,
        "section_16_answer_level_csv_output_path": section_16_answer_level_csv_output_path,
        "section_16_strategy_summary_csv_output_path": section_16_strategy_summary_csv_output_path,
        "section_16_overall_profile_csv_output_path": section_16_overall_profile_csv_output_path,
        "section_16_intermediate_reasoning_csv_output_path": section_16_intermediate_reasoning_csv_output_path,
        "section_16_examples_json_output_path": section_16_examples_json_output_path,
        "section_16_report_output_path": section_16_report_output_path,
    }
